### [110] [Beyond wheelchairs and blindfolds: Investigating disability stereotypes in T2I models with INCLUDE-BENCH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08515)
*Sophia Lichtenberg,Albert Gatt,Judith Masthoff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了INCLUDE-BENCH，首个大规模评估文生图模型中残疾偏见的数据集，包含119K张图像和多种偏见维度，发现轮椅描绘过度主导、残疾条件生成多样性不足、刻板印象对齐更强等问题，并引入了SCM分数来量化刻板印象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型的社会偏见研究主要关注性别、肤色和文化表征，且局限于职业关联，而残疾维度被系统性忽视。当前的评估方法缺乏与社会学刻板印象定义的对齐，无法原则性地评估对残疾人群体的表征伤害。

Method: 构建INCLUDE-BENCH基准：基于多维度偏见提示设计生成119K图像，涵盖静态和动态上下文，评估15个开源和2个闭源模型，引入基于刻板印象内容模型（SCM）的量化评分。

Result: (1) 行动障碍和默认残疾提示在所有模型中主要生成轮椅描绘；(2) 残疾条件生成始终缺乏多样性；(3) 刻板印象描绘表现出更强的残疾-文本对齐；(4) 引入的SCM分数表明文生图模型反映了现实世界的刻板印象关联。

Conclusion: 残疾偏见在文生图模型中普遍存在且形式单一，现有模型未能公平表征残疾人群体的多样性，INCLUDE-BENCH为系统性评估和缓解这类偏见提供了工具和基线。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have been shown to exhibit social biases. Prior work has mainly focused on gender, skin tone, and cultural representation within restricted occupational associations, and emerging benchmarks increasingly incorporate these dimensions. However, disability remains systematically underexplored. Current evaluation practices often fail to align with sociologically grounded definitions of stereotyping, limiting principled assessment of representational harms toward people with disabilities (PWD). To address this, we introduce INCLUDE-BENCH, the first large-scale benchmark for evaluating disability-related bias in T2I models. INCLUDE-BENCH comprises 119K generated images based on prompt design across multiple bias dimensions and both static and dynamic contexts. We evaluate 15 open-source and 2 closed-source models. Our key findings reveal that: (1) mobility-impaired and default disability prompts predominantly yield wheelchair depictions across all models; (2) disability-conditioned generations consistently exhibit less diversity; (3) stereotypical portrayals demonstrate stronger disability-text alignment; and (4) we introduce the Stereotype Content Model (SCM) Score, demonstrating that T2I models reflect real-world stereotypical associations.

</details>


### [111] [Whareformer: Learning to Track What is Where in Long Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08537)
*Jacob Chalk,Saptarshi Sinha,Dima Damen,Yannis Kalantidis,Diane Larlus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于学习的OSNOM任务解决方案Whareformer，使用Transformer架构结合可更新记忆和轨迹分配模块，在仅56个训练视频上实现三个数据集260个长视频上的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OSNOM任务需要追踪被相机佩戴者移动的物体，即使在物体离开视野或严重遮挡时仍需维持其位置知识，现有方法缺乏学习驱动的高效解决方案。

Method: 提出Whareformer，包含两个组件：(1)可更新记忆模块存储已建立轨迹；(2)前馈式轨迹分配模块将观测与现有轨迹关联。模型联合推理物体外观(what)和更新的3D位置(where)，并使用专用的New Track token来识别新物体。

Result: 在56个训练视频上训练，在EPIC-KITCHENS-100（未见视频）、IT3DEgo和HD-EPIC三个数据集的260个长测试视频上达到SOTA，显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 使用相对距离和演化轨迹表示的设计选择使得Whareformer能够在小规模训练数据上实现有效的跨数据集泛化。

Abstract: The recently established 'Out of Sight, Not out of Mind' (OSNOM) task for egocentric videos focuses on tracking objects that are moved by the camera wearer, online, maintaining knowledge of instance locations throughout the video even when they leave the field of view or become heavily occluded. In this paper, we propose the first learning-based solution to the OSNOM task: Whareformer, a transformer-based model with two components: an updatable memory of established tracks and a track assignment module that associates observations with existing tracks in a feed-forward manner. Whareformer jointly reasons over evolving object appearance (what) and updated 3D location (where), and employs a dedicated New Track token to reason about novel objects.
  Thanks to its design choices of using relative distances and evolving track representations, Whareformer is trained on a small set of 56 videos but achieves SOTA performance on 260 long test videos from three datasets: EPIC-KITCHENS-100 (unseen videos), IT3DEgo, and HD-EPIC, with significant absolute improvements over prior work.

</details>


### [112] [VocaDet: Sample-Driven Open-Vocabulary Object Detection and Segmentation via Visual Tokenization and Vector Database Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08541)
*ZhiXin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VocaDet提出了一种基于样本驱动的开放词汇目标检测与分割框架，通过将视觉特征离散化为视觉词汇并存储在可扩展的向量数据库中，无需模型重训练即可从用户提供的正负样本中学习目标概念，实现了连续可扩展的识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇检测方法依赖文本提示、有限的视觉示例或昂贵的特征匹配过程，难以扩展到大规模且持续增长的目标库。

Method: 使用DINOv3提取视觉特征，通过自适应凝聚聚类生成多粒度视觉token；结合位置去偏表征和空间拓扑信息构建可扩展的目标记忆向量数据库；引入背景过滤机制减少冗余检索。

Result: 在UA-DETRAC数据集上实现了无需传统检测器训练的有效开放词汇检测性能，且支持随正负样本积累的连续可扩展识别。

Conclusion: VocaDet通过视觉词汇化和检索式识别范式，为开放词汇检测提供了一种可扩展的样本驱动解决方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection and segmentation aim to recognize arbitrary objects beyond predefined categories. Although recent vision-language and reference-based approaches have significantly advanced this field, they often rely on text prompts, limited visual examples, or expensive feature matching procedures, making them difficult to scale to large and continuously expanding object repositories. In this work, we propose VocaDet, a sample-driven open-vocabulary object detection and segmentation framework that learns object concepts directly from user-provided positive and negative sample collections without model retraining. The key idea is to transform continuous visual representations into discrete visual vocabularies and perform efficient retrieval-based recognition through a scalable vector database. Specifically, we employ DINOv3 as the visual feature extractor and apply agglomerative clustering with adaptive clustering sensitivity to generate multi-granularity visual tokens. These visual tokens, together with position-debiased representations and spatial topology information, are stored as expandable object memories in a vector database. During inference, query images are converted into visual tokens and efficiently matched against the stored object memories for object localization and segmentation. Furthermore, a background filtering mechanism is introduced to remove frequently occurring background patterns and reduce redundant retrieval operations in practical fixed-camera scenarios. Experiments on the UA-DETRAC dataset demonstrate that VocaDet achieves effective open-vocabulary detection performance without conventional detector training, while supporting continuously expandable recognition capability as additional positive and negative samples are accumulated.

</details>


### [113] [Switch-Reasoner: Learn When to Think in Multitask Mixtures via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08572)
*Yiyang Fang,Pei Fu,Jinjie Li,Jian Liang,Wenke Huang,Ruijie Luo,Shaojie Zhang,Jian Luan,Yi R. Fung,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Switch-Reasoner框架，通过GRPO和双层调控机制让MLLM自适应选择直接回答或推理后回答，在11个多模态任务上实现更优的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs采用固定的先推理后回答范式，在异构多任务场景中效率低下：简单输入无需显式推理，困难输入则受益于推理。同时，后训练中学习何时推理存在不稳定性，不平衡的rollout会导致模型倾向于始终推理或始终直接回答。

Method: 提出Switch-Reasoner框架：1) 将思考视为虚拟工具调用，允许模型选择直接回答模式或显式推理后再回答；2) 采用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）进行训练；3) 引入双层调控机制——全局层面平衡两种模式的使用率，样本层面基于两种选择的相对收益提供监督信号。

Result: 在11个多模态任务上的实验表明，Switch-Reasoner减少了不必要的推理，同时保持了强劲的性能，实现了更优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 通过自适应推理模式选择，可以有效解决MLLM在多任务场景下的效率问题，双层调控机制稳定了学习过程，避免了推理策略崩溃问题。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often follow a fixed Think-then-Answer paradigm, which is inefficient in heterogeneous multitask settings because simple inputs may not require explicit reasoning while difficult ones can benefit substantially from it. Learning when to think is also unstable during post-training, where imbalanced rollouts can drive the model toward always-thinking or always-direct behavior. We propose Switch-Reasoner, a GRPO-based framework that learns to adaptively select reasoning modes for MLLMs. It treats thinking as a virtual tool invocation and allows the model to either answer directly or invoke explicit reasoning before answering. To stabilize this decision, we introduce a dual-level regulation mechanism that balances the overall use of Thinking Mode and Direct Mode while providing sample-level supervision based on the relative benefit of the two choices. Experiments on 11 multimodal tasks show that Switch-Reasoner reduces unnecessary reasoning while maintaining strong performance, achieving a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [114] [When Structured Sparse Autoencoders Learn Consistent Concepts Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08605)
*Weiduo Liao,Yunqiao Yang,Ying Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化稀疏自编码器（S²AE），通过注意力和空间先验对图像块进行分组，并引入结构化稀疏正则化（组间互斥稀疏+组内群组稀疏），在保持高重构保真度（EV>99%）的同时，提升VLM视觉特征的概念一致性与单语义性，mIoU提升6.06%，l0范数降低60.81，跨模态单语义性得分提升2.37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉-语言模型（VLM）中，传统稀疏自编码器（SAE）难以学习模态一致的概念，视觉模态中的概念常呈现碎片化覆盖（即不连续的区域），导致概念表示质量不佳。

Method: 提出结构化稀疏自编码器（S²AE）：1) 基于Transformer注意力相似性和空间邻近性对图像块进行分组；2) 引入结构化稀疏正则化，包含组间互斥稀疏（促进不同组间的概念解缠）和组内群组稀疏（促进组内概念一致性），引导SAE的潜在神经元专注于不同且语义扎根的概念。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct模型上，语义对齐（mIoU）平均提升6.06%，表示效率（l0范数）降低60.81，重构保真度（Explained Variance>99%）接近完美。跨模态分析表明，视觉结构先验增强了神经元的单语义性，语义一致性平均提升3.08%，单语义性得分平均提升2.37%。

Conclusion: S²AE通过在视觉模态中引入结构化稀疏先验，有效解决了VLM中概念碎片化问题，实现了更连贯、更解缠的多模态表示。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a promising technique for mechanistic interpretability by learning a set of sparse latent features in large models, each of which encodes a distinct concept. However, in vision-language models (VLMs), vanilla SAEs struggle to learn modality-consistent concepts, with concepts often exhibiting fragmented coverage (i.e., disjoint regions) in the visual modality. To address this challenge, we propose a Structured Sparse AutoEncoder ($S^2AE$) that enforces concept consistency from both semantic and spatial perspectives in the visual modality. Specifically, we group image patches based on Transformer attention similarity and spatial proximity, and introduce a structured sparsity regularization when training the vanilla SAE. The regularization consists of exclusive sparsity for inter-group concept disentanglement and group sparsity for intra-group concept consistency, which drives the latent neurons by SAEs to specialize in distinct, semantically grounded concepts. Evaluated on the \texttt{Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct} model, the method achieves 6.06% average improvement in semantic alignment (mIoU) and 60.81 in representational efficiency (lower l0 norm) while maintaining near-perfect reconstruction fidelity with an Explained Variance above 99%. Cross-modal analysis further demonstrates that $S^2AE$ enhances neuronal monosemanticity by this visual structural prior, achieving a 3.08% average gain in semantic consistency and a 2.37% average gain in monosemanticity scores for both modalities of multimodal features, thereby fostering more coherent and disentangled representations.

</details>


### [115] [Do Transformations Reveal the Truth? Generative Residual Learning for Generalized AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08674)
*Kutub Uddin,Nusrat Tasnim,Awais Khan,Mohammad Umar Farooq,Khalid Malik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GenRes和GenRes++框架，通过神经张量网络进行生成式残差学习，建模原始样本与变换样本之间的细粒度关系特征，并结合可学习的注意力机制聚合多变换关系特征，以提升AI生成图像的跨域检测性能。实验表明，GenRes++在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展使得深度伪造媒体极具真实性，带来了 misinformation、数字身份盗用、欺诈及舆论操纵等重大威胁。由于生成方法的多样性和伪影的细微性，AI生成图像检测面临可靠挑战。

Method: 提出GenRes框架，采用神经张量网络进行生成式残差学习，建模原始样本与变换样本间的细粒度关系特征；进一步提出GenRes++，引入可学习的注意力机制聚合多变换样本的关系特征，使模型聚焦于最具信息量的线索。两者均使用PE-Core作为特征提取器，提供通用且语义丰富的嵌入。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的综合实验表明，GenRes++方法在性能上优于现有检测方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的GenRes/GenRes++框架通过残差关系学习与注意力聚合，有效提升了AI生成图像检测的跨域泛化能力，能够检测未知生成方法产生的图像。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative AI has enabled the creation of highly realistic deepfake media, posing significant threats, including misinformation, digital identity theft, fraud, and manipulation of public opinion. AI-generated image (AIGI) detection is reliably challenging due to the diversity of generative methods and the subtle artifacts they leave behind. In this work, we propose GenRes, a novel framework for generative residual learning via a neural tensor network, which models fine-grained relational features between original and transformed samples to enhance generalization. To address scenarios involving multiple generative transformations, we introduce GenRes++, which employs a learnable attention mechanism to aggregate relational features across multiple transformed samples and enables the model to focus on the most informative cues. Both models leverage PE-Core as a feature extractor, providing generalized and semantically rich embeddings that improve cross-domain performance and enable the detection of AIGI generated by unseen methods. Comprehensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed GenRes++ approach outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [116] [Multi-Resolution Feature Stem for Diabetic Retinopathy lesion segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08679)
*Indranil Dutta,Taehee Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现DR病灶分割中提高输入分辨率对微动脉瘤有利但会意外降低大出血灶的性能，并提出多分辨率特征茎（Multi-Resolution Feature Stem）架构解决该分辨率权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DR病灶尺寸差异巨大（从微小动脉瘤到大出血灶和渗出），高分辨率对细节有利但可能对大病灶产生负面影响，现有研究缺乏对这一矛盾现象的实证分析。

Method: 系统实验多个架构（U-Net, UNet++, Vision Transformers, DeepLabV3+）在512×512和1024×1024分辨率下的表现；提出Multi-Resolution Feature Stem输入级金字塔结构，与UNet++骨干网络集成，并行处理多尺度信息。

Result: 发现了高分辨率提升微动脉瘤性能但意外降低了出血灶分割性能的反直觉现象；提出的Multi-Resolution Feature Stem架构有效解决了该权衡问题。

Conclusion: 证实了DR病灶分割中分辨率对不同类型的差异化影响，提出的参数高效架构能在捕获细粒度细节的同时不损失上下文信息，为处理多尺度病变提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Diabetic Retinopathy (DR) is a leading cause of preventable blindness worldwide, requiring automated lesion segmentation using deep learning models for early detection and monitoring. However, DR lesions vary dramatically in size from tiny microaneurysms to large hemorrhages and exudates. This variability creates conflicting demands on the model architecture and input resolution, posing a challenge for effective design. This work investigates the impact of input resolution on different lesion types. Through systematic experimentation with multiple architectures (U-Net, UNet++, Vision Transformers, DeepLabV3+) at $512 \times 512$ and $1024 \times 1024$ resolutions, we identify a critical, counter-intuitive phenomenon where increasing input resolution has opposing effects on different lesion types. We demonstrate that while higher resolution is essential for resolving fine-grained microaneurysms, it can unexpectedly degrade performance on larger hemorrhages. This finding challenges the common assumption that higher resolution is uniformly beneficial. To address this, we propose a novel Multi-Resolution Feature Stem, an input-level pyramid integrated with a UNet++ backbone. This architecture processes multiple scales in parallel, capturing fine-grained details without sacrificing contextual information. This work contributes crucial empirical evidence of this complex, resolution-dependent behavior and a practical, parameter-efficient architecture that successfully resolves this trade-off.

</details>


### [117] [SAM-MT: Real-Time Interactive Multi-Target Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08688)
*Ruiqi Shen,Chang Liu,Henghui Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAM-MT在SAM2基础上提出了一种解耦多目标处理与延迟的交互式框架，通过显式查询、解耦掩码注意力和稀疏记忆机制，实现了与目标数量无关的实时多目标视频分割（10目标>36 FPS），同时保持SAM2的强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VOS方法在单目标场景表现优异，但扩展到多目标时需为每个目标复制单目标处理流程，导致帧率下降且延迟随目标数量增加而无限增长，无法满足实时多目标分割的需求。

Method: 基于SAM2构建SAM-MT交互式框架：1）使用显式查询并行表示不同目标，同时共享全局上下文表征；2）采用解耦掩码注意力（Decoupled Masked Attention）保持各目标身份独立，避免跨目标干扰；3）引入稀疏记忆机制实现稳定的时序演化；4）设计专门的遮挡处理和重叠预防策略。

Result: SAM-MT成功将延迟与目标数量解耦，在10个目标场景下达到>36 FPS的实时速度（与单目标基线相当），同时保持了SAM2原有的鲁棒视频分割性能。

Conclusion: SAM-MT通过并行化多目标处理架构解决了多目标VOS中延迟随目标数增长的关键瓶颈，在保持高性能的同时实现了恒定实时速度，为实际应用中的多目标视频分割提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Modern Video Object Segmentation (VOS) involves tracking and segmenting user-specified targets. While recent approaches have achieved remarkable performance in single-target scenarios, extending them to multi-target settings typically involves replicating the single-target processing for each individual object, resulting in reduced frame rates (FPS) with unbounded latency as target count increases. Built upon Segment Anything 2 (SAM2), we propose SAM-MT, which addresses this by transforming the model into an interactive framework for real-time Multi-Target video segmentation. SAM-MT uses explicit queries to represent different individual targets, in parallel with a shared representation for global context. It employs decoupled masked attention to keep individual identities distinct from cross-target interference, and sparse memory for stable temporal evolution, along with specialized strategies for occlusion handling and overlap prevention. SAM-MT successfully decouples latency from the number of targets, achieving real-time speed on par with single-target baselines (>36 FPS for 10 targets) while maintaining SAM2's robust video segmentation performance.

</details>


### [118] [HumanForge: A Human-Centric Deepfake Video Benchmark with Multi-Agent Forgery Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08705)
*Wenbo Xu,Zhimin Chen,Xiaojie Liang,Hengrui Liu,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了HumanForge，一个统一的大规模多范式人类中心视频伪造数据集，以及Gen2Anno，一种基于LangGraph的模块化主动多智能体管线，用于自动化生成和标注高质量视频伪造样本，并揭示了现有检测方法在该数据集上的零样本泛化和细粒度推理挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频伪造基准主要关注换脸或全局文生视频，忽略了人-物/人-人交互和多模态对齐等关键维度，无法满足日益严峻的人类中心视频伪造检测需求。

Method: 提出Gen2Anno管线，基于LangGraph协调6个专门智能体（包括源分析、MoE参考分析、闭环取证验证等），自动生成18K+高保真视频片段并生成结构化对比全标注（含二分类决策、细粒度伪影类别、时空定位）。

Result: 使用传统检测器和大型多模态模型进行广泛基准测试，发现HumanForge在零样本泛化和细粒度推理方面存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: HumanForge填补了人类中心视频伪造检测基准的空白，揭示了现有方法在复杂交互场景下的不足，数据集和代码将开源。

Abstract: Rapid advancements in video diffusion models and temporal editing tools have enabled the generation of highly realistic human-centric videos, posing unprecedented challenges to digital content forensics. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on either face-swapping or global text-to-video synthesis, overlooking the crucial dimensions of human-object or human-human interactions and multi-modal alignment. To address these limitations, we introduce HumanForge, a unified, large-scale, and multi-paradigm human-centric video forgery dataset. To construct and annotate this dataset without labor-intensive manual labeling or hallucinated monolithic prompts, we propose Gen2Anno, a modular active multi-agent pipeline built on LangGraph. Gen2Anno coordinates six specialized agents-ranging from source profiling to MoE-based reference analysis and closed-loop forensic verification-to generate over 18K high-fidelity video segments and produce structured, contrastive omni-annotations containing binary decisions, fine-grained artifact categories, and spatio-temporal localization. Extensive benchmarks using state-of-the-art traditional detectors and Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) demonstrate the significant challenges of zero-shot generalization and fine-grained reasoning on HumanForge. Code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [119] [LTM: Large-scale Terrain Model for Wildfire-prone Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08711)
*Xiao Fu,Yue Hu,Meida Chen,Peter Anthony Beerel,Barath Raghavan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态重建框架，利用过时的数字高程模型（DEM）作为几何先验，结合基于物理的像素-像素对齐方法，实现高效、高保真的3D地形重建，适用于野火应急响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火风险评估需要大面积高精度的3D地形图，但现有方法存在成本高（机载LiDAR）、更新频率低或性能差（基于图像的方法受限于稀疏视觉特征和有限重叠）的问题。

Method: 提出多模态重建框架，利用过时DEM作为几何先验；核心创新是基于物理的像素-像素对齐，在图像与DEM数据之间建立对齐，省去昂贵的特征匹配步骤，降低计算复杂度。

Result: 基于真实野火区域的大范围地形模拟器验证，方法在保持实时性能的同时生成高保真深度图，在重建精度和计算效率上显著优于现有技术。

Conclusion: 该方法为野火响应提供了一种可扩展的低成本解决方案，通过融合过时DEM先验与实时图像重建，有效平衡了精度、效率和成本。

Abstract: Accurate 3D terrain maps are essential for emergency response when assessing wildfire hazards. However, wildfire-prone regions often span vast areas where conventional reconstruction methods underperform. Airborne LiDAR systems provide high-resolution terrain data, but they are expensive and infrequently updated. Image-based methods offer a lower-cost alternative, but struggle due to sparse visual features and limited image overlap. We propose a multi-modal reconstruction framework leveraging outdated Digital Elevation Models (DEMs) as geometric priors for image-based 3D reconstruction. Our key innovation is physics-based pixel-pixel alignment between images and DEM data, dramatically reducing computational complexity by eliminating expensive feature matching procedures. To validate our approach, we developed a large-terrain simulator based on a real wildfire-prone area, generating realistic images enabling a comprehensive evaluation. Given posed images and legacy DEMs, our method produces high-fidelity depth maps while maintaining real-time performance. We find significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency over existing techniques, offering a scalable solution for wildfire response.

</details>


### [120] [Pose-to-Biomechanics: Bridging 3D Human Pose Estimation and Biomechanical Attribute Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08725)
*Ayda Eghbalian,Kevin Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BioModule是一个轻量级插件式时间transformer，可附加于任何3D姿态估计器之后，从标准17关节3D骨架预测生物力学属性，无需修改上游模型，实现了视觉姿态估计与生物力学分析之间的模块化桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体姿态估计主要优化几何关键点精度，而康复、运动科学、工效学和临床运动分析等实际应用需要描述身体如何运动、负载和激活的生物力学量，两者之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出BioModule，一种轻量级插件式时间transformer，可接在任何3D姿态估计器下游，从标准17关节3D骨架预测生物力学属性；构建了Human3.6M与Human3.6Mplus对齐的大规模数据集，建立并验证了两个坐标系之间的解剖对应关系，实现帧级别跨模态监督。

Result: 在7种先进3D姿态估计器上进行了系统基准测试，首次分析了上游姿态估计质量如何传播到下游生物力学预测 fidelity，结果表明BioModule作为紧凑模块化桥梁的有效性。

Conclusion: BioModule定位为连接视觉姿态估计与生物力学意义人体运动分析的紧凑模块化桥梁，可扩展现有姿态估计器至物理可解释的运动分析。

Abstract: Recent progress in 3D human pose estimation has made markerless recovery of skeletal motion increasingly accurate and scalable. However, most pose estimators remain optimized for geometric keypoint accuracy, while many real-world applications in rehabilitation, sports science, ergonomics, and clinical movement analysis require biomechanical quantities that describe how the body moves, loads, and activates. In this work, we propose BioModule, a lightweight plug-in temporal transformer that attaches downstream of any 3D pose estimator and predicts biomechanical attributes from standard 17-joint 3D skeletons. BioModule is estimator-agnostic and requires no modification of the upstream pose model, enabling existing pose estimators to be extended toward physically interpretable motion analysis.
  To train and evaluate BioModule, we construct a large-scale aligned dataset pairing Human3.6M video and 3D keypoints with the biomechanical label space of Human3.6Mplus. We establish and verify anatomical correspondence between coordinate systems of the two datasets, enabling frame-accurate cross-modal supervision. Using this aligned supervision, BioModule predicts biomechanical quantities. We further benchmark BioModule across seven state-of-the-art 3D pose estimators, providing the first systematic analysis of how upstream pose estimation quality propagates to downstream biomechanical prediction fidelity. The results position BioModule as a compact, modular bridge between vision-based pose estimation and biomechanically meaningful human motion analysis.

</details>


### [121] [WaspMOT: A Benchmark for Long-Term Multi-Object Tracking of Trichogramma Wasps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08729)
*Tomasz Stanczyk,Yuan Gao,Hardik Agarwal,Seongroo Yoon,Tiantao Zhang,Vincent Calcagno,Francois Bremond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WaspMOT是一个面向长期多目标跟踪的基准数据集，包含约12,000帧的赤眼蜂视频序列，所有个体始终存在，评估了五种跟踪算法后发现在完美检测下仍存在严重的轨迹碎片化问题，表明长期身份保持仍是重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOT基准大多基于短时序视频，无法充分评估长期身份保持能力，因此需要一个新的基准来填补这一评估缺口。

Method: 构建了10个长序列（每段~12,000帧）的赤眼蜂跟踪数据集，提供MOTChallenge格式标注和Oracle检测，在闭环场景下用统一协议评估了ByteTrack、BoT-SORT、C-BIoU、OC-SORT和McByte五种跟踪方法，并提出了基于空间轨迹拼接的简单基线。

Result: 所有方法在完美检测条件下均出现显著轨迹碎片化，空间轨迹拼接基线持续提升性能，表明当前方法在长期关联上仍有巨大改进空间。

Conclusion: WaspMOT揭示了传统短时基准无法观察到的长期跟踪局限性，为研究长期身份关联提供了新的测试平台。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) has achieved strong performance on benchmarks dominated by short video sequences. However, such datasets do not adequately evaluate long-term identity preservation, where objects must be tracked consistently over extended durations. We introduce WaspMOT, a benchmark designed to address this gap through long-duration tracking of Trichogramma wasps in controlled ecological experiments. The dataset contains 10 sequences of approximately 12,000 frames each (over 8 minutes at 25 FPS), with dense MOTChallenge annotations and oracle detections to isolate association performance.
  Unlike existing benchmarks, WaspMOT forms a closed-set tracking scenario where all individuals remain present throughout the sequence, requiring consistent identity assignment across thousands of frames despite abrupt jumps, occlusions, and highly similar appearance. We establish a benchmark by evaluating five tracking-by-detection methods, including ByteTrack, BoT-SORT, C-BIoU, OC-SORT, and McByte, under a unified protocol. Results show that all methods suffer from significant trajectory fragmentation, highlighting the difficulty of long-term identity preservation even with perfect detections. A simple spatial tracklet stitching baseline consistently improves performance, indicating that substantial gains remain possible.
  WaspMOT provides a new benchmark for studying long-term association and reveals limitations of current tracking approaches that are not observable on conventional datasets. The benchmark will be made publicly available at the project repository: https://github.com/tstanczyk95/WaspMOT/ .

</details>


### [122] [OpenCoF: Learning to Reason Through Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08763)
*Xinyan Chen,Ziyu Guo,Renrui Zhang,Dongzhi Jiang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenCoF框架，包含OpenCoF-17K推理视频数据集和微调模型Wan-CoF，通过时间帧链（CoF）推理机制提升视频生成模型的推理能力，并探索了视觉和文本推理token对空间/时间推理的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型主要在通用视频语料上训练，缺乏多样化的监督信号和专门设计来支持链式帧（CoF）推理能力，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 构建包含11个任务族、17K样本的推理视频数据集OpenCoF-17K，对Wan2.2-I2V-A14B进行微调得到Wan-CoF模型，并引入视觉推理token和文本推理token分别捕获低层视觉线索和高层语义先验，用于空间和时间推理。

Result: 在四个视频推理基准上，Wan-CoF相比基线Wan2.2-I2V-A14B取得了显著提升。通过性能对比和注意力分析，揭示了推理token在不同模型深度、去噪步数、空间和时间维度上的贡献。

Conclusion: 更强的视频推理能力需要两方面：广泛的时间监督信号，以及用于组织中间推理状态的显式机制。

Abstract: Reasoning has become a core capability for large models, especially when reliable decisions require understanding logical consequences. Recent video generation models offer a reasoning path distinct from previous Chain-of-Thought (CoT): reasoning can unfold through temporally connected frames, known as Chain-of-Frame (CoF) reasoning. However, existing video generators are primarily trained on general video corpora, still lacking diverse supervision and dedicated designs for CoF reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce OpenCoF, a framework comprising the OpenCoF-17K dataset, a reasoning video dataset spanning 11 task families, and Wan-CoF, a fine-tuned video model for studying whether diverse temporal supervision improves CoF behavior. Across four video reasoning benchmarks, Wan-CoF achieves considerable gains over the Wan2.2-I2V-A14B baseline. Building on this, we empirically explore more advanced designs for CoF capabilities, i.e., equipping the model with visual and textual reasoning tokens. This mechanism respectively captures low-level visual cues and high-level semantic priors for spatial and temporal reasoning. Through performance comparisons and attention analysis, we examine how these tokens contribute across model depth, denoising steps, space, and time. Our results suggest that stronger video reasoning requires both broad temporal supervision and explicit mechanisms for organizing intermediate reasoning state. We open-source the dataset, model, and code to facilitate future research on reasoning-oriented video generation.

</details>


### [123] [Enhancing In-context Panoramic Generation via Geometric-aware Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08765)
*Haoran Feng,Ruiyang Zhang,Longyi Zhang,Dizhe Zhang,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Canvas360 提出两阶段框架（几何感知预训练+下游任务微调），构建了包含100万高质量全景样本的Canvas360Dataset，通过并行深度生成、速度循环填充和相似性损失正则化等技术，实现了统一的上下文全景生成框架，支持风格迁移、修复、外绘和编辑等多种任务，在全景生成保真度上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏大规模高质量的全景上下文任务训练数据，且难以同时支持风格迁移、修复、外绘和编辑等多种下游任务，模型对全景图像的几何一致性和全局连贯性建模不足。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）几何感知预训练，通过并行深度生成、速度循环填充和相似性损失正则化提升全景先验；2）下游任务微调，通过token级拼接实现统一的上下文全景生成框架，支持多种任务。

Result: 在全景专用FAED指标上表现优异，在多项定量评估中达到有竞争力或领先水平，全景图像保真度显著提升。

Conclusion: Canvas360通过几何感知预训练和统一框架有效解决了全景上下文生成中的几何一致性和任务泛化问题，在任务覆盖和建模灵活性上超越此前方法。

Abstract: In this work, we present Canvas360, a two-stage framework for in-context panoramic generation that combines geometry-aware pretraining with downstream task-specific fine-tuning. To address the lack of large-scale, high-quality training data tailored to in-context panoramic tasks, we propose Canvas360Dataset, a collection of 1M high-quality paired panoramic samples for style transfer, inpainting, outpainting, and editing, enabling effective supervision across diverse in-context generation scenarios. On the modeling side, Canvas360 enhances text-to-panorama generation through parallel depth generation, velocity circular padding, and similarity loss regularization, enabling the model to learn geometry-aware representations, capture object distortion details, and improve geometric consistency and global coherence. Furthermore, empowered by strong panoramic priors, Canvas360 enables a unified in-context panoramic generation framework that supports diverse downstream tasks via token-level concatenation, surpassing prior methods in both task coverage and modeling flexibility. Extensive experiments show that Canvas360 improves panoramic image fidelity, achieving particularly strong performance on the panorama-specific FAED metric and competitive or leading results across the reported quantitative evaluations. More information can be found on our project page: https://zry000.github.io/Canvas360/

</details>


### [124] [OPSD-V: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Post-Training Few-Step Autoregressive Video Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08766)
*Hongyu Liu,Chun Wang,Feng Gao,Xuanhua He,Yue Ma,Ziyu Wan,Yong Zhang,Xiaoming Wei,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OPSD-V，一种基于on-policy自蒸馏的后训练范式，用于改进少步自回归视频扩散模型的长视频生成质量，减少误差累积并增强运动动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步AR视频生成器虽能低延迟生成长视频，但在长自回归推理中仍存在误差累积和运动动态减弱的问题。

Method: OPSD-V引入真实长视频数据作为时序上下文，学生模型按推理时的自回归方式生成，教师模型在学生访问的去噪状态上评估，但使用更干净的AR一致性时序缓存（将旧历史替换为真实视频上下文），提供密集的去噪级校正目标。

Result: 在Self-Forcing和LongLive模型上实验，视觉质量、运动动态和VBenchLong分数一致提升；用户研究中66.0%的总体偏好选择OPSD-V（排除平局后为82.5%）。

Conclusion: OPSD-V在不改变采样器、去噪步数或推理缓存机制的前提下，有效缓解长视频生成退化问题。

Abstract: We propose OPSD-V, an on-policy self-distillation paradigm for post-training few-step autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models. Existing few-step AR video generators can produce long videos with low latency, but still suffer from error accumulation and weakened motion dynamics during long autoregressive rollout. OPSD-V reduces long-horizon degradation while preserving the original few-step inference path. The key idea is to introduce real long-video data as temporal context during training and use it to provide dense trajectory-level supervision. Specifically, the student follows the exact inference-time rollout, generating each chunk conditioned on its own previously generated KV cache. In parallel, the teacher is evaluated at the same student-visited denoising states, but uses a cleaner AR-consistent temporal cache in which older history can be replaced by real-video context. This provides dense denoising-level corrective targets under on-policy AR cache dynamics, without changing the sampler, number of denoising steps, or inference-time cache mechanism. We apply OPSD-V to representative few-step AR video models, including Self-Forcing and LongLive. Experiments show consistent improvements in visual quality, motion dynamics, and VBenchLong scores. A user study with 10 participants comparing 20 video pairs shows that OPSD-V is preferred over the base models in 66.0% of overall-preference judgments (82.5% excluding ties).

</details>


### [125] [Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning for Panoramic Outdoor Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08769)
*Weijian Chen,Weibo Yao,Yuhang Zhang,Xiaolin Tang,Guo Wang,Weijun Zhang,Xitong Gao,Yihao Chen,Hongde Qin,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PanoLOG，一个针对大规模户外场景的全景3DGS重建框架，采用几何与梯度引导的分区策略（G²PS），通过粗到精的两阶段训练实现高保真渲染与可扩展的块并行训练，并构建了首个大规模户外全景重建基准Pano360。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将3DGS扩展到大规模户外场景在数据采集和计算上代价高昂。虽然采用全景图像（ERP）可减少采集成本，但其360°全视场导致传统基于局部相机视锥的分区策略失效，块优化退化为全局训练。因此需要一种针对全景图像特性的可扩展分区方案。

Method: 提出两阶段粗到精框架PanoLOG：1）全局粗阶段利用天空球建模和全景单目深度监督获得可靠几何；2）精炼阶段提出G²PS（Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning Strategy），通过视差驱动的不确定性构建自适应边界体，并基于梯度重要性评分分配相机，实现块并行训练。

Result: 在构建的Pano360大规模户外全景数据集上，G²PS达到了最先进的渲染质量，同时保持了可扩展的块并行训练效率。

Conclusion: G²PS有效解决了全景图像下3DGS分区训练的退化问题，为大规模户外场景的全景重建提供了高效且高质量的解决方案，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Scaling 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to large outdoor scenes is costly in both data acquisition and computation. Adopting panoramic images with equirectangular projection (ERP) can reduce capture effort via their full $360^{\circ}$ field of view, yet the resulting omnipresent visibility invalidates existing partitioning strategies that rely on local camera frustums, causing block-wise optimization to degenerate into global training. Thus, we propose PanoLOG, a two-stage coarse-to-fine framework equipped with a Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning Strategy tailored for large-scale panoramic 3DGS reconstruction. In the global coarse stage, PanoLOG leverages sky-sphere modeling and panoramic monocular depth supervision for reliable geometry, while in the refinement stage, G$^2$PS builds adaptive bounding volumes via parallax-driven uncertainty and assigns cameras via gradient-based importance scoring. Furthermore, we construct Pano360, the first benchmark on large-scale panoramic dataset for outdoor scene reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G$^2$PS achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while maintaining scalable, block-parallel training. Our models, training code, and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [126] [LongE2V: Long-Horizon Event-based Video Reconstruction, Prediction, and Frame Interpolation with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08770)
*Cheng-De Fan,Chun-Wei Tuan Mu,Chen-Wei Chang,Chin-Yang Lin,Kun-Ru Wu,Yu-Chee Tseng,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongE2V通过微调预训练视频扩散模型，联合处理事件相机的视频重建、预测和帧插值任务，采用自回归展开、自适应上下文切换、重编码对齐与交叉残差校正以及事件体素密度增强等技术，在真实基准上实现了超越现有方法的性能，展现出优异的时间一致性和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏事件流中恢复高质量视频极具挑战性：回归方法会导致纹理模糊，而现有生成模型在长时间序列中缺乏稳定性。

Method: 1）利用预训练视频扩散先验，微调基础视频模型以联合处理事件视频重建、预测和帧插值；2）自回归展开（Autoregressive Unrolling）与自适应上下文切换（Adaptive Context Switching）缓解长序列时间漂移；3）重编码对齐与交叉残差校正（Reencoding Alignment with Cross Residual Correction）确保帧插值时的双向一致性；4）事件体素密度增强（Event Voxel Density Augmentation）提升对不同传感器分辨率的鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实基准数据集上，LongE2V在事件视频重建、预测和帧插值三项任务中均超越现有最先进方法，展现出优异的时间一致性和零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: LongE2V通过引入视频扩散先验和多种时序稳定性技术，有效解决了事件到视频重建中的纹理模糊和长期漂移问题，在多项任务中达到SOTA。

Abstract: Recovering high-quality video from sparse event streams is a challenging task. Regression methods often blur textures, while existing generative models struggle with long-term stability. We propose LongE2V, a novel approach that leverages pre-trained video diffusion priors to jointly handle event-based video reconstruction, prediction, and frame interpolation. By fine-tuning a foundational video model, our approach achieves high data efficiency and superior perceptual quality. We introduce Autoregressive Unrolling and Adaptive Context Switching to mitigate temporal drift in extremely long sequences. We also propose Reencoding Alignment with Cross Residual Correction to ensure precise bidirectional consistency during frame interpolation. Furthermore, Event Voxel Density Augmentation ensures robustness across varying sensor resolutions. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that LongE2V outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all three tasks, exhibiting exceptional temporal coherence and zero-shot generalization. Project page: https://cdfan0627.github.io/LongE2V-page/

</details>


### [127] [ZipDepth: Bringing Lightweight Zero-Shot Monocular Depth Anywhere, on Any Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08771)
*Fabio Tosi,Luca Bartolomei,Matteo Poggi,Stefano Mattoccia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZipDepth是一个仅6.1M参数的轻量级单目深度估计网络，通过重参数化编码器-解码器设计与大规模多域知识蒸馏，在五类基准上实现了零样本准确率与部署效率的最佳平衡，性能接近参数多50倍的基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度估计中，基础模型零样本泛化能力强但计算开销大，无法部署于嵌入式/移动平台；轻量级模型虽存在，但局限于单域自监督学习范式，在域偏移下性能退化严重。因此需要一种兼顾零样本精度与部署效率的轻量级方案。

Method: 结合高效的重参数化编码器-解码器架构与基于基础模型的大规模多域知识蒸馏，在大型多域训练集上进行训练，模型参数量仅6.1M。

Result: 在五个基准测试上，ZipDepth在轻量级模型中实现了零样本精度与部署效率的最佳权衡，性能接近参数多50倍的基础模型，且能在从服务器GPU到功耗受限设备上实时运行。

Conclusion: ZipDepth通过紧凑设计与知识蒸馏策略，有效弥合了轻量级模型与基础模型在零样本深度估计精度上的差距，为资源受限场景下的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has seen remarkable progress through foundation models achieving robust zero-shot generalization, yet their computational demands place them far beyond the reach of embedded and mobile platforms. Lightweight alternatives exist, but have been developed almost exclusively within single-domain, self-supervised paradigms, failing silently under domain shift. We present ZipDepth, a compact monocular depth network that bridges this gap by combining an efficient reparameterizable encoder-decoder with large-scale knowledge distillation from a foundation model over a large multi-domain training set. Comprising just 6.1M parameters, ZipDepth runs at real-time rates from server GPUs to power-constrained devices, achieving the best trade-off between zero-shot accuracy and deployment efficiency among lightweight models across five benchmarks, taking a significant step towards the accuracy of foundation models with 50x more parameters.

</details>


### [128] [Wat3R: Underwater 3D Geometry Learning without Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08772)
*Jiangwei Ren,Xingyu Jiang,Zijie Song,Wei Xu,Hongkai Lin,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Wat3R，一种跨域半监督学习框架，通过教师-学生架构和跨视图一致性损失，无需水下标注数据即可将前馈3D重建模型从空气域适应到水下场景，并构建了Water3D评估数据集，在深度估计和点云重建任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境存在光衰减、散射等挑战，且缺乏大规模高质量3D标注数据，现有依赖密集标注的方法在水下场景中不可行。

Method: 采用教师-学生架构的跨域半监督学习框架；设计跨视图一致性损失，利用多视图几何线索补偿当前视图因水体衰减和散射导致的信息退化；构建Water3D数据集用于几何任务评估。

Result: 在水下多视图深度估计和点云重建任务上，Wat3R优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: Wat3R无需任何水下标注数据即可有效实现空气到水下场景的3D重建模型跨域适应。

Abstract: Estimating 3D geometry in underwater environments presents unique challenges due to light attenuation, scattering, and the absence of large-scale, high-quality 3D annotations. Pioneering methods rely on massive dense annotations that are impractical in underwater settings. In this paper, we propose Wat3R, a cross-domain semi-supervised learning framework designed to adapt feed-forward 3D reconstruction models from air to underwater scenes. Uniquely, our method eliminates the need for any annotated underwater data following a teacher-student architecture, that learns robust geometry representations merely on abundant unlabeled real underwater video footage. We also design a cross-view consistency loss that leverages geometric cues from other views to compensate for the information degradation in the current view caused by water attenuation and scattering. Furthermore, considering the lack of comprehensive evaluation benchmarks, we construct Water3D, a diverse dataset covering various water bodies and underwater scenarios, designed for geometric task evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that Wat3R outperforms current state-of-the-art methods in underwater multi-view depth estimation and point cloud reconstruction. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/LSXI7/Wat3R .

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Controllability-Aware Adversarial Examples Against LLM-Based Network Traffic Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07739)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可控性感知的黑盒迁移攻击框架，用于评估基于LLM的网络流量分类器的对抗鲁棒性。通过将流量特征划分为直接可控（DC）、间接可控（IC）和不可控（UC）三类，仅扰动DC特征，生成三种对抗样本并迁移攻击至7个LLM和2个传统ML目标。实验发现LLM的迁移脆弱性显著但依赖数据集和比较对象，梯度/分数攻击优于贪婪攻击，且跨代理验证一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）正被探索用于网络入侵检测分类器，但其在现实攻击者约束下的对抗鲁棒性尚不清楚，现有研究缺乏对LLM在网络流量分类场景下对抗攻击的系统性评估。

Method: 提出可控性感知的黑盒迁移攻击框架：1) 根据网络通信语义将流特征分为DC、IC、UC三类；2) 仅允许扰动DC特征，冻结IC/UC特征；3) 使用共享XGBoost代理模型生成有限差分PGD、贪婪坐标式和NES三种对抗样本；4) 迁移攻击至7个LLM和2个传统ML目标，在5个IDS基准数据集（1999-2022）上评估。

Result: 在27个有效LLM配置和超过50万对抗样本上：1) LLM的迁移脆弱性显著但依赖数据集和比较对象；2) 相比LightGBM，LLM在RT-IoT2022和CIC-IDS-2018上更脆弱，在NSL-KDD和UNSW-NB15上相当，在HIKARI-2021上更鲁棒；3) 相比平均ML基线，LLM在所有5个数据集上ASR更高；4) 梯度/分数攻击迁移性优于贪婪攻击；5) 跨代理（树/神经网络/线性代理）验证结果一致，约束违规率为0%。

Conclusion: LLM作为网络入侵检测分类器存在显著的对抗迁移脆弱性，但程度因数据集和比较对象而异。提出的可控性感知框架有效确保了攻击的现实可行性（约束违规率为0%），且攻击迁移性在不同代理模型上表现一致，说明发现具有普适性而非特定于XGBoost。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored as network intrusion detection classifiers, but their adversarial robustness under realistic attacker constraints remains unclear. We present a controllability-aware black-box transfer framework for LLM-based network traffic classifiers. The framework partitions flow features into directly controllable (DC), indirectly controllable (IC), and uncontrollable (UC) groups according to network communication semantics, then restricts perturbations to DC features while freezing IC/UC features. Using a shared XGBoost surrogate, we generate finite-difference PGD, greedy coordinate-wise, and NES adversarial examples and transfer them to seven LLM targets and two conventional ML targets across five IDS benchmarks from 1999 to 2022. Across 27 valid LLM configurations and over 500,000 adversarial examples, we find that LLM transfer vulnerability is substantial but dataset- and comparator-dependent. Compared with LightGBM, LLMs are more vulnerable on RT-IoT2022 and CIC-IDS-2018, comparable on NSL-KDD and UNSW-NB15, and less vulnerable on HIKARI-2021; compared with the averaged ML baseline, LLMs show higher ASR on all five datasets. We further observe a consistent cross-architecture transfer hierarchy: gradient- and score-based perturbations transfer more effectively than greedy perturbations across all 27 LLM cells and 9/10 ML cells. Cross-surrogate validation with tree, neural, and linear surrogates yields similar LLM ASR, reducing evidence that the findings are XGBoost-specific. Constraint violation rate is 0\% by construction.

</details>


### [130] [Forensic Schema for Psychological Manipulation in Cyber Fraud: LLM-Driven Victim Reports Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07751)
*Zikai Alex Wen,Corrazon Ogot,Juan Li,Yan Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含35个问题、4大类别的新型网络犯罪取证分类模式，新增11个心理操控指标和加密货币证据字段。通过对10,994份受害者报告进行LLM驱动标注，发现每种主要诈骗类型具有统计上显著的心理操控特征（Cramer's V最高达0.790），但区块链特定标识符几乎缺失，建议采用AI辅助的受害者问询流程来弥补证据细节缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络犯罪分类模式仅捕捉联系元数据和金融交易，忽略了犯罪者使用的心理操控技术，导致取证分析不完整。

Method: 基于已有取证基础构建含11个操控指标和加密货币证据字段的四类别35问题取证分类模式；利用LLM对10,994份受害者报告进行自动标注；与两名人类标注者进行信度验证（平均LLM-人类κ=0.69，与标注者间κ=0.68匹配）；通过基于理由的证据审计评估细节充分性。

Result: 每种主要诈骗类型具有统计上显著不同的操控特征（Cramer's V最高0.790）；操控技术的检测可靠，但受害者叙述在支持每个"Yes"答案的可操作细节上差异巨大；区块链特定标识符几乎完全缺失。

Conclusion: 最直接的改进路径是采用AI辅助的受害者问询流程，结合分类模式指导的追问问题来弥补证据细节缺口；该分层标注策略为其他取证文本领域的LLM提取提供了可复用模板。

Abstract: Existing cybercrime classification schemas capture contact metadata and financial transactions but omit the psychological manipulation techniques perpetrators employ. We present a forensic schema (four categories, 35 questions) adding 11 manipulation indicators and cryptocurrency evidence fields to established forensic foundations. Applied to 10,994 victim reports via large language model (LLM)-driven annotation and validated against two human annotators (mean LLM-human $κ= 0.69$, matching inter-annotator $κ= 0.68$), the schema revealed a statistically distinct manipulation profile for each major fraud type (Cramer's $V$ up to $0.790$). A rationale-based evidence audit nonetheless exposed a forensic detail gap: detection of manipulation techniques was reliable, but victim narratives varied widely in the actionable detail supporting each Yes answer, and blockchain-specific identifiers were nearly absent. These findings point to AI-assisted victim intake with schema-informed follow-up questions as the most direct way to close the gap. The tiered annotation strategy also provides a reusable template for LLM-based extraction from other forensic text domains.

</details>


### [131] [ScopeJudge: Cost-Aware Pre-Execution Gating for Offensive Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07774)
*Shane Caldwell,Max Harley,Ads Dawson,Michael Kouremetis,Vincent Abruzzo,Will Pearce*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM安全智能体的工具调用越界问题，提出预执行门控机制——用一个轻量可信的LLM裁判在工具调用执行前进行审核。构建了包含4,897个工具调用（7.7%越界）的ScopeJudge基准，由专业渗透测试人员标注，评估了8种裁判模型和5种上下文策略，发现静态策略不足以识别越界，并推荐了成本敏感和召回优先两种部署方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在攻防安全领域执行任务时，一个越界的工具调用可能破坏用户约定的边界范围（scope），导致生产中断或漏洞奖励失效。与固定安全策略不同，scope由用户请求中的意图动态定义，且攻防场景具有对抗性——同一工具调用是否越界取决于目标和上下文，无法预先枚举。因此需要一种能根据用户请求实时判断工具调用是否越界的机制。

Method: 提出预执行门控（pre-execution gating）：在强智能体生成工具调用后、执行之前，由一个轻量可信的LLM裁判审核并决定接受或拒绝。构建ScopeJudge基准：从攻防任务中收集4,897个工具调用（7.7%越界），由专业渗透测试人员标注，一致性较高（Fleiss kappa=0.64）。评估8种裁判模型在5种上下文策略（从仅静态策略到完整原始对话记录）下的表现，绘制成本-准确率帕累托前沿。

Result: 静态策略由于无法感知用户请求，裁判召回率接近零，证明scope信息存在于用户请求中，必须进行请求条件化监控。因错过越界调用的代价大于误拒绝，分别报告精确率、召回率和F1值。推荐两个操作点：成本敏感配置和召回优先配置（用于高风险场景）。专家一致性参考F1=0.78。

Conclusion: 静态策略在scope执行上存在结构性不足，请求条件化的实时监控是必要的。ScopeJudge数据集可用于支持自主安全智能体的实时监控和可扩展监督。

Abstract: As LLM agents take on offensive security work, a single out-of-scope tool call can breach a client's engagement boundary, disrupt production, or void a bug-bounty finding. Unlike a fixed safety policy, the boundary that matters is declared in the user's request and must be inferred from intent. That challenge is sharpened by the adversarial nature of offensive security: the same tool call is in or out of scope depending not on the action itself but on the target it touches and the context in which it runs, which no fixed policy can enumerate in advance. We study pre-execution gating: a cheap, trusted LLM judge inspects each call proposed by a strong, swappable agent, and accepts or rejects it before it runs. We introduce ScopeJudge, a benchmark of 4,897 tool calls (7.7% scope violations) from agent trajectories on tasks engineered to tempt agents out of scope and labeled at the call level by professional penetration testers, with substantial inter-grader agreement (Fleiss kappa = 0.64) that sets an expert agreement reference point of F1 = 0.78. We evaluate eight judge models under five transcript strategies, varying how much context the judge sees, from the static policy alone to the full raw transcript, and chart the resulting cost-accuracy Pareto frontier. We find that a static policy is structurally insufficient for scope enforcement: blind to the user's request, judge recall collapses to near zero, confirming that scope lives in the request and that request-conditioned monitoring is necessary. Because a missed violation costs more than a spurious rejection, we report precision, recall, and F1 separately and recommend two operating points: a cost-sensitive configuration and a recall-first one for high-stakes deployments. We release the ScopeJudge dataset to support real-time monitoring and scalable oversight of autonomous security agents.

</details>


### [132] [Open Models, Open Risks: Measuring Unsafe Generation in Text-to-Image Models In the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07827)
*Peilin Han,Yang Liu,Yilong Yang,Jingchun Zhang,Teng Li,Jianfeng Ma,Zhuo Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对200+个Hugging Face上的开源T2I模型进行了大规模野外安全性实证研究，发现现有基于检测器的越狱指标因语义漂移和生成伪影而高估实际风险，为此提出了高级ASR指标。结果表明，多数下游模型即使没有显式护栏也保留了相当的安全性，但存在部分高风险模型（包括明确NSFW导向和表面良性的模型），其不安全行为仅通过系统评估才暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I安全研究多基于受控实验室环境中的少量标准模型，对快速增长的真实T2I生态系统的安全状态缺乏了解。同时，基于检测器的越狱指标不适用于野外评估，且野外风险不仅来自对抗提示，还来自不安全的发布实践和模型衍生。

Method: 1) 提出Advanced ASR指标，以解决检测器指标因语义漂移和生成伪影导致的误报问题；2) 使用改进指标对200+个Hugging Face上的野外T2I模型，在三种代表性越狱攻击下进行大规模评估。

Result: 多数下游模型即使没有显式后处理护栏仍保留一定安全性，安全性退化并非普遍均匀；但识别出一组高风险模型，包括明确NSFW导向的发布以及表面良性但仅通过系统评估才暴露不安全行为的模型。

Conclusion: 野外T2I模型的安全性状态复杂：既非普遍不安全，也存在隐蔽的高风险。论文进一步追溯了高风险模型的发布上下文，并向Hugging Face报告了相关案例。

Abstract: Existing safety studies on text-to-image (T2I) jailbreaks are largely conducted in controlled in-the-lab settings, typically on a small number of canonical models. As a result, the current safety status of the rapidly growing in-the-wild T2I ecosystem remains unclear. This uncertainty is amplified by two factors: existing detector-based metrics are designed for controlled evaluation, and in-the-wild risks may arise not only from adversarial prompting, but also from unsafe release practices and unsafe model derivatives.
  In this paper, we present a large-scale empirical study of in-the-wild T2I safety through the lens of jailbreak. We first show that detector-only jailbreak metrics substantially overestimate practical risk over in the wild due to semantic drift and generation artifacts, and we introduce Advanced ASR to better capture semantically valid and visually plausible unsafe generation. Using this refined metric, we evaluate 200+ in-the-wild T2I models from Hugging Face under three representative jailbreak attacks. Our results show that many downstream models retain a non-trivial degree of safety even without explicit post-hoc safeguards, indicating that safety degradation in the wild is neither universal nor uniform. At the same time, we identify a set of high-risk models, including explicitly NSFW-oriented releases as well as seemingly benign models whose unsafe behavior is only exposed through systematic evaluation. We further trace these models to their release context and report high-risk cases to Hugging Face.

</details>


### [133] [Closed-Loop Dynamic Validator Node Scaling in Private Substrate Blockchains Using Takagi-Sugeno Fuzzy Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07901)
*Thandile Nododile,Ayinde M. Usman,Clement N. Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Takagi-Sugeno模糊推理系统的自适应验证节点管理控制器，能够根据实时区块链参数（区块生产时间、区块大小、活跃节点数）输出效率评分和扩缩容建议（增加/维持/减少节点），并在10节点Substrate区块链网络上验证了其稳定性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私有区块链网络采用固定节点配置，无法适应动态变化的工作负载：节点过多造成资源浪费，节点过少则降低区块生产速度和最终确认效率，且最优验证节点数量难以确定。

Method: 构建Takagi-Sugeno模糊推理系统，使用三角形隶属函数覆盖三个语言变量，包含完整的27条规则库和乘积t-范数聚合；创新性地基于测试床实际运行范围而非理论极值进行隶属函数的经验重校准。

Result: 在4、7、10个活跃节点的配置上，控制器产生不同的运行画像；闭环实验中控制器能双向自主调整验证节点参与数量，从两个方向收敛到相同稳定均衡；相比三种阈值基线的对比方法，扩缩容振荡更少，区块生产时间相当。

Conclusion: TS模糊推理能够支持私有区块链部署中的自主验证节点管理，其稳定扩缩容行为是阈值方法无法比拟的。

Abstract: Private blockchain networks run with fixed node configurations that cannot adapt to changing workload conditions. Too many nodes serving a light workload waste resources; too few nodes facing heavy demand slow block production and degrade finalisation. The right validator count is hard to determine, as it depends on overlapping factors that shift over time. This paper presents a Takagi-Sugeno (TS) fuzzy inference system that reads live blockchain parameters (block production time, block size, and active node count) and outputs a continuous efficiency score alongside a scaling recommendation: Scale Up, Maintain, or Scale Down. The controller uses triangular membership functions across three linguistic variables, evaluated through a complete 27-rule base with product t-norm aggregation. A key contribution is an empirical recalibration of the membership functions, anchoring linguistic terms to the observed operating range of the testbed rather than to theoretical extremes. The system is evaluated on a 10-node Substrate blockchain network storing real smart water meter data hashes from the Queensland Government open data portal. Statistical analysis across configurations of 4, 7, and 10 active nodes confirms that the controller produces distinct operational profiles reflecting each configuration's provisioning state. In closed-loop experiments, the controller autonomously adjusts validator participation in both directions, activating validators under rising load and removing them under over-provisioning, converging to the same stable equilibrium from both directions. Compared against three threshold-based baselines, it shows fewer scaling oscillations while maintaining comparable block production times. Results show that TS fuzzy inference can support autonomous validator management in private blockchain deployments, with stable scaling behaviour threshold approaches cannot match.

</details>


### [134] [Mechanistic Interpretability of LLM Jailbreaks via Internal Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07903)
*Anupam Wagle,Ifrat Ikhtear Uddin,Chaowei Zhang,Longwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于内部计算图（ICG）的机制性框架，通过构建和对齐干净与攻击提示下的计算图，系统揭示对抗攻击如何抑制安全相关组件、涌现攻击特征并重路由计算路径。该框架实现从描述性归因到因果诊断的转变，支持不变/抑制/涌现结构分解、漏洞模式识别及因果干预，实验证明结构偏差与不安全行为强相关，靶向干预可提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖输入-输出行为或归因方法分析LLM对抗攻击，对扰动如何改变模型内部推理机制的了解有限，不安全行为的底层机理认知不足。

Method: 提出配对的内部计算图框架：构建干净与攻击提示下的结构化因果交互图，对齐后分解为不变、抑制、涌现三种结构，识别漏洞模式，并在节点、路径、子图上进行因果干预以评估其对攻击成功的贡献。

Result: 在多个开源LLM和多种对抗/越狱基准上，内部计算图的结构偏差与不安全行为强相关；针对识别出的漏洞模式进行靶向干预可有效提升模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 内部计算图为理解、诊断和缓解LLM漏洞提供了原则性基础，实现了从描述性归因到因果诊断的范式转变。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable capabilities but remain highly vulnerable to adversarial prompts and jailbreak attacks. Existing approaches primarily analyze these failures through input-output behaviors or attribution methods, offering limited insight into how adversarial perturbations alter the model's internal reasoning. Consequently, the mechanisms underlying unsafe or incorrect behaviors remain poorly understood. We introduce a mechanistic framework for diagnosing LLM vulnerabilities using paired internal computation graphs, which represent prompt-specific inference as structured causal interactions among latent features. By constructing and aligning computation graphs for clean and attacked prompts, we reveal that adversarial attacks induce systematic transformations of internal reasoning, including suppression of safety-relevant components, emergence of attack-specific features, and rerouting of computation paths. Building on this representation, we propose a unified framework that (i) decomposes computation into invariant, suppressed, and emergent structures, (ii) identifies recurring vulnerability motifs associated with failure modes, and (iii) performs causal interventions on nodes, paths, and subgraphs to directly evaluate their contributions to attack success. This enables a transition from descriptive attribution to causal diagnosis of model failures. Experiments across multiple open-source LLMs and diverse adversarial and jailbreak benchmarks demonstrate that structural deviations in internal computation graphs strongly correlate with unsafe behaviors. Furthermore, targeted interventions on identified vulnerability motifs improve model robustness, establishing internal computation graphs as a principled foundation for understanding, diagnosing, and mitigating LLM vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [135] [KS-CFA: Control-Flow Attestation via Symbolic Replay Against Control-Flow Bending Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07926)
*Zhanyu Sha,Konstantinos Markantonakis,Carlton Shepherd,Amir Rafi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: KS-CFA是一种新的控制流证明方案，通过将符号执行与输入源控制流相关变量识别相结合，在不枚举大量测量空间或依赖底层硬件修改的情况下，检测包括控制流弯曲在内的各类攻击。实现基于RISC-V Keystone TEE，在QEMU和FPGA平台上的开销分别为6.8-20.5x和6.7-32.2x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制流证明方案面临控制流弯曲攻击难以检测的问题，且存在测量空间枚举量大、攻击覆盖范围有限、依赖底层硬件修改等不足。

Method: 结合符号执行与选择性识别输入源控制流相关变量，证明方在TEE内记录控制流轨迹及决定运行时变量的外部输入，验证方通过单一路径符号执行重放报告路径，预测每次转移并定位指示攻击的偏离。

Result: 在QEMU上证明方开销为6.8-20.5倍，在FPGA上为6.7-32.2倍；验证过程无需路径或值的枚举。

Conclusion: KS-CFA能有效检测四种转移类型上的CFB攻击，无需枚举测量空间或硬件修改，在可接受的性能开销内实现了更强的安全保证。

Abstract: Control-flow attestation (CFA) enables a remote entity to verify program execution on a target device by monitoring control-flow behaviour at runtime. However, control-flow bending (CFB) attacks remain difficult to detect, where an adversary steers execution along legal edges of the program's control-flow graph by corrupting branch flags, loop counters, and other runtime data. Existing solutions impose significant drawbacks: they require enumerating vast measurement spaces, cover only a reduced subset of attacks, or rely on low-level hardware modifications. In this work, we present KS-CFA, a new CFA scheme that detects CFB attacks across four transfer types (indirect calls, conditional and indirect jumps, and returns) without those costs. To this end, we combine symbolic execution and selective identification of input-sourced control-flow dependent variables: a strict subset of control-flow-relevant state whose values are directly read from external input. The proving device records, inside a trusted execution environment (TEE), a control-flow trace and the external inputs that determine relevant run-time variables. The verifier then replays the reported path through single-path symbolic execution, predicting each transfer and localising divergences that signal an attack. We implement and evaluate KS-CFA using the RISC-V Keystone TEE and Embench-IoT on QEMU and a Rocket-based FPGA platform (NiteFury II). Prover-side overhead relative to unattested execution ranges from 6.8-20.5x on QEMU and 6.7-32.2x on the FPGA, and verification requires no path or value enumeration.

</details>


### [136] [Who Broke the System? Failure Localization in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07989)
*Yufei Xia,Anjun Gao,Yueyang Quan,Zhuqing Liu,Minghong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentLocate是一个针对基于大语言模型的多智能体系统的故障定位框架，能够将失败归因到具体的智能体和最早的关键决策步骤，通过LLM判断机制、多角度验证和置信度感知聚合，并辅以轻量级微调来提升归因质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在分布式协作执行复杂任务时，由于长程交互和智能体行为紧密耦合，系统级故障难以诊断——无法确定哪个智能体负责以及轨迹在何时首次出现不可逆的错误方向。

Method: AgentLocate结合了基于LLM的判断机制与独立评估者的多角度验证，通过置信度感知策略聚合评估结果，并利用聚合反馈对判断器进行轻量级微调以提升归因质量。

Result: 在两个互补基准测试上的实验表明，AgentLocate在识别责任智能体和故障步骤方面 consistently 优于现有故障定位方法，同时在token使用和运行时间方面保持高效。

Conclusion: AgentLocate有效解决了多智能体系统中的故障定位难题，为分布式LLM系统的诊断提供了可靠且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-agent systems enable complex problem solving through coordinated reasoning and action, but their distributed structure also introduces new challenges in diagnosing system-level failures. When an execution fails, identifying which agent is responsible and at what point the trajectory first becomes irreversibly misdirected is difficult due to long-horizon interactions and tightly coupled agent behaviors. In this paper, we study the problem of failure localization in LLM-based multi-agent systems and present AgentLocate, a framework that attributes failures to both a specific agent and the earliest decisive step. AgentLocate combines an LLM-based judging mechanism with multi-perspective verification by independent evaluators, whose assessments are aggregated using a confidence-aware strategy. The resulting feedback is further used to adapt the judge through lightweight fine-tuning, improving attribution quality. We evaluate AgentLocate on two complementary benchmarks covering diverse tasks, agent configurations, and trajectory lengths. Experimental results show that AgentLocate consistently outperforms existing failure localization methods in identifying both responsible agents and failure steps, while remaining efficient in terms of token usage and running time.

</details>


### [137] [Beware What You Autocomplete: Forensic Attribution of Backdoored Code Completions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08011)
*Anjun Gao,Yueyang Quan,Zhuqing Liu,Minghong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CodeTracer是一个能够将恶意代码补全追溯到后门微调数据的取证框架，在多种攻击场景下展现出高准确率、低误报率和强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的代码补全系统易受后门攻击，而现有的防御技术难以检测和缓解复杂的自适应后门攻击，亟需能够追溯恶意行为来源的取证方法。

Method: CodeTracer在仅依赖微调语料库和误补全事件报告的情况下，从被攻破的输出中提取结构化行为指纹，将搜索范围缩小至语义相关的代码样本，并利用基于LLM的推理将不安全逻辑归因到特定的后门数据。

Result: 在三个代表性漏洞案例和十种后门攻击、十六个竞争基线的广泛评估中，CodeTracer持续实现了高取证准确率、低误报率，并对自适应攻击表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CodeTracer是一种有效的后门攻击归因取证框架，能够在现实部署约束下准确追溯恶意代码补全的来源。

Abstract: Large language models have enabled powerful code completion systems that assist developers by predicting subsequent lines of code. However, these models remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where malicious fine-tuning data covertly implants unsafe behaviors. Despite advances in defensive techniques, adaptive and sophisticated backdoor attacks still evade detection and mitigation. We present CodeTracer, a forensic framework that traces malicious code completions back to the backdoor fine-tuning data responsible for them. Operating under realistic post-deployment constraints, CodeTracer relies solely on the fine-tuning corpus and the reported miscompletion event. It extracts a structured behavioral fingerprint from the compromised output, narrows the search to semantically relevant code samples, and employs LLM-based reasoning to attribute unsafe logic to specific backdoor data. Extensive evaluations across three representative vulnerability cases and ten backdoor attacks, along with sixteen competitive baselines, demonstrate that CodeTracer consistently achieves high forensic accuracy, low false identification rates, and strong robustness against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [138] [Degree-Constrained Interval Optimization for Minimax Polynomial Approximation in Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08042)
*Jiheon Woo,Donggyun Ryu,Yongjune Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布感知的区间优化方法，结合极小极大多项式与域扩展函数（DEF/DEP）来平衡同态加密下非多项式激活函数的近似精度与鲁棒性，并推导了可解析计算的代理目标函数及实现误差上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在同态加密的隐私保护推理中，非多项式激活函数需用多项式近似。极小极大多项式（Remez算法）的逼近区间选择存在权衡：区间越宽，对大幅值输入的鲁棒性越好，但固定度数下的最大逼近误差越大。现有方法缺乏对这一权衡的分布感知优化，需要一种能结合输入分布信息来最优选择逼近区间的方法。

Method: 1. 将逼近区间选择建模为分布感知的区间优化问题，以最小化关于预激活分布的重构均方误差（MSE）为目标。2. 引入域扩展函数（DEF）及其同态加密可实现的多项式版本（DEP）来近似区间外的截断操作，抑制多项式外推。3. 推导可解析计算的DEF代理目标函数，量化区间内极小极大误差与区间外截断误差之间的权衡。4. 通过实现误差分解和上界分析，将理想DEF目标与可实现的DEP联系起来。

Result: 1. 建立了分布感知的区间优化框架，将逼近区间选择问题形式化。2. 推导了DEF-based可解析代理目标函数，能有效捕捉区间内逼近误差与区间外截断误差的权衡。3. 给出了DEP实现的误差分解与上界，建立了从理想DEF到实际DEP的理论桥梁。

Conclusion: 该工作为同态加密下非多项式激活函数的多项式近似提供了一种理论驱动的分布感知方法，能够根据实际输入分布自适应选择最优逼近区间，并通过DEF/DEP机制有效控制区间外行为，在隐私保护推理中实现精度与鲁棒性的更好平衡。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) enables privacy-preserving inference under arithmetic constraints that restrict encrypted evaluation to additions and multiplications. As a result, non-polynomial activation functions must be replaced by polynomial approximations. Among polynomial approximation methods, minimax approximation, typically computed by the Remez algorithm, is a standard approach because it minimizes the maximum approximation error over a given design interval. For minimax polynomial design, the approximation interval is a critical hyperparameter: a wider interval improves robustness to large-magnitude inputs while increasing the minimax approximation error under a fixed degree budget. In this paper, we formulate this trade-off as a distribution-aware interval optimization problem, where the approximation interval is chosen to minimize the mean-squared error (MSE) with respect to the pre-activation distribution of interest. To effectively control outside-interval inputs, we combine minimax polynomials with domain extension functions (DEFs) and their HE-realizable polynomial counterparts, domain extension polynomials (DEPs), which approximate a clipping operation outside the design interval and thereby suppress uncontrolled polynomial extrapolation. We first derive an analytically tractable DEF-based proxy objective that captures the trade-off between within-interval minimax approximation error and outside-interval clipping error. We then connect this idealized objective to HE-realizable DEP constructions through an implementation-error decomposition with an accompanying upper bound.

</details>


### [139] [zkComposer: Decomposing Proof Construction to Scale zkML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08095)
*Pawan Kumar Sanjaya,Christina Giannoula,Valdy Oktavian,Mehdi Saeedi,Gabor Sines,Gururaj Saileshwar,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: zkComposer提出模块化证明构建框架，将zkML推理证明分解为独立子证明，通过共享承诺加密链接相邻子证明，在不改变底层密码原语的前提下，在CNN和GPT-2上分别实现最高3.25倍和6.84倍的证明时间与响应时间加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有zkML系统面临证明生成成本过高的问题，且证明生成存在并行性受限——增加线程数并不能有效减少证明时间，原因是现有系统依赖单一的整体证明计算（monolithic proof），为整个ML模型构建单一证明。

Method: 提出模块化证明构建框架zkComposer，将zkML正确推理证明分解为独立子证明，每个子证明覆盖推理计算的一个子集（如连续层的子集），相邻子证明通过边界层激活值的共享承诺进行密码学链接，无需额外的链接证明或修改底层密码原语。

Result: 在CNN工作负载上，相比zkCNN，证明时间和响应时间降低最多3.25倍；在GPT-2上，沿模型层划分时相比zkGPT降低最多4.83倍；在GPT-2上同时沿模型层和输入序列划分时，相比zkGPT降低最多6.84倍。

Conclusion: zkComposer通过解锁额外的并行维度（在现有证明核并行性之外），显著降低了zkML的证明生成时间，且保持了与整体证明相同的安全保证。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge machine learning (zkML) enables a server to perform verifiable inference while keeping model parameters private from the client. However, existing zkML systems incur prohibitive proof-generation costs. We observe that proof generation exhibits limited parallelism; that is, prover time does not decrease significantly as the number of threads increases. This limitation is because existing systems rely on monolithic proof computation, constructing a single proof for the entire machine learning model. We introduce zkComposer, a modular proof-construction framework that unlocks an additional dimension of parallelism, in addition to the parallelism in existing proof kernels. zkComposer decomposes the zkML proof of correct inference into independent sub-proofs, each covering a subset of the computation for inference e.g., each independent sub-proof can cover a subset of contiguous layers in the ML model. Adjacent sub-proofs are cryptographically linked through shared commitments to the activations from the boundary layer. zkComposer provides the same guarantees as the monolithic proof without requiring additional linking proofs or changes to the underlying cryptographic primitives. We implement zkComposer and evaluate it on three CNNs and GPT-2. We show that, on CNN workloads, zkComposer reduces prover time and response time by up to 3.25x relative to zkCNN [1]. On GPT-2, zkComposer reduces these times by up to 4.83x relative to zkGPT [2], when partitioning along the model layers. When partitioning across both model layers and input sequences in GPT-2, we show that zkComposer reduces prover time and response time by up to 6.84x relative to zkGPT [2].

</details>


### [140] [Securing Autonomous Vehicle Systems via Twin-Aware Federated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08137)
*Zifan Zhang,Minghong Fang,Dianwei Chen,Zhuqing Liu,Prashant Khanduri,Xianfeng Yang,Anupam Das,Yuchen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SecAgg防御框架，通过数字孪生排练学习、历史聚合参数与中心梯度筛选机制，抵御联邦强化学习中的投毒攻击，保障自动驾驶决策安全，并提供收敛性理论保证与数字孪生环境验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦强化学习在动态车辆环境中能实现多智能体协作学习且不共享原始数据，但投毒攻击严重威胁其安全性与可靠性，尤其在安全攸关的自动驾驶领域该脆弱性尚未被充分探索，攻击者可通过注入恶意系统参数破坏全局控制模型，引发潜在危险。

Method: 提出SecAgg防御框架，集成数字孪生用于排练式学习，利用历史聚合模型参数与选定的中心梯度，确保仅聚合良性数据，从而有效缓解恶意智能体的影响。

Result: 提供了在投毒攻击下SecAgg收敛性能的理论保证；利用开发的高速公路数字孪生环境验证了对抗条件下自动驾驶车辆控制的有效性。

Conclusion: SecAgg通过历史强化与安全聚合机制，能有效提升联邦强化学习系统在安全攸关驾驶场景下抵御投毒攻击的鲁棒性，理论与实验共同验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Federated reinforcement learning (FRL) is crucial for enabling collaborative learning across multiple agents without sharing raw data, thereby enhancing privacy and scalability in the decision-making process within dynamic vehicular environments. However, poisoning attacks pose a significant threat to the security and reliability of FRL-based systems, particularly in safety-critical autonomous driving, where this vulnerability remains largely unexplored. These attacks can compromise the global control model by subtly injecting malicious system parameters, leading to potential hazards. To counter these challenges, we present \alg (\underline{Sec}ure \underline{A}ggregation with \underline{p}oisoning-\underline{p}revention and historical reinforcement) as a defensive framework aimed at enhancing the robustness of FRL systems designed for safety-critical driving scenarios. \alg strategically integrates digital twins for rehearsal-based learning and leverages historical aggregated model parameters along with a selected central gradient to ensure that only benign data is aggregated, effectively mitigating the influence of malicious agents. Theoretical guarantees are provided for the convergence performance of \alg in the presence of poisoning attacks. We also validate the effectiveness of \alg using developed digital twins that model realistic highway environments to evaluate the control of autonomous vehicles under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [141] [Prismata: Confining Cross-Site Prompt Injection in Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08147)
*Corban Villa,Alp Eren Ozdarendeli,Sijun Tan,Raluca Ada Popa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Prismata，一种针对自主网页代理的上下文最小权限防御机制，通过动态信任推导和机械隔离来抵御提示注入攻击，在不依赖开发者标注的情况下显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主网页代理在执行浏览任务时，会将自然语言解释为指令，这使得第三方和用户生成内容能够通过提示注入劫持代理。核心挑战在于，推导任务特定的安全策略需要对页面结构进行推理，而页面结构与攻击者内容纠缠在一起，传统安全机制难以应对。

Method: Prismata采用动态信任推导，为页面内容生成权限标签，并借鉴经典完整性模型提供结构性隔离保证，确保标签只能降级且误标边界可控。通过内容编辑和限制代理能力来实现机械隔离，且无需开发者注释，适用于长尾网站。

Result: 在多种已发表的网页代理攻击（包括自适应变体）测试中，Prismata大幅降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了良性的任务可用性。

Conclusion: Prismata通过上下文最小权限原则，有效解决了网页代理面临的安全威胁，为自主代理的安全部署提供了可行的防御方案。

Abstract: Autonomous web agents promise to automate everyday browsing tasks, but inherit one of the web's oldest attack surfaces. Cross-Site Scripting proved that mixing trusted and untrusted content is dangerous, even on benign pages. Agents resurface this risk by interpreting natural language as instructions, allowing third-party and user-generated content to hijack the agent via prompt injection. The core challenge is that deriving a task-specific security policy requires reasoning over page structure that is entangled with the attacker's content.
  We present Prismata, a defense enforcing contextual least privilege for web agents, constraining both what the agent sees and what it can do. Prismata's dynamic trust derivation produces permission labels for page content, with structural confinement guarantees, inspired by classical integrity models, that bound any labeling errors so that labels can only decrease in privilege and mislabelings are bounded. Prismata's mechanical confinement enforces these labels by redacting content and restricting agent capabilities. Importantly, these mechanisms require no developer annotations, so Prismata supports the long tail of websites. Across recent published web agent attacks, including adaptive variants, Prismata substantially reduces attack success while preserving benign task utility.

</details>


### [142] [Out of Sight: Compression-Aware Content Protection against Agentic Crawlers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08180)
*Xuefei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CAPE框架，通过在内容中注入对人类不可见但对智能体压缩器有效的扰动，利用上下文压缩这一常规操作作为防御层，保护高价值文本免受基于LLM的智能体侵害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体具备推理、摘要和记忆能力，对在线内容构成新威胁。现有防御（如访问控制、注入式防御）易被绕过或损害可读性，亟需新的保护方案。

Method: CAPE从可访问的替代压缩器中提取破坏性种子扰动，通过先验引导的进化和偏好校准的候选优先级排序，将其适配到仅查询的目标压缩器，在低查询预算下实现有效保护。

Result: 在三种内容类型和四种压缩设置下，CAPE相比最强基线将信息损失提升高达75.8%，且保护内容与原始内容视觉上不可区分。在LangGraph智能体工作流和GitHub Copilot中验证了其可迁移性。

Conclusion: 揭示上下文压缩可作为新的防御层，推动智能体时代的内容保护研究。

Abstract: The rise of LLM-based agents with reasoning, summarization, and memory capabilities has created a new threat surface for online content that conventional defenses fail to address. Existing defenses like access controls can be circumvented by agents mimicking ordinary browsers, and injection-based defenses often degrade human readability. In this paper, we revisit the agent pipeline and identify context compression, which agents routinely invoke to fit context budgets, as a critical yet overlooked defense layer. We propose CAPE, a framework that protects high-value textual content by injecting invisible perturbations without changing its human-visible surface form, thereby inducing severe information loss during agent compression. CAPE extracts disruptive seed perturbations from an accessible surrogate compressor, then adapts them to query-only target compressors through prior-guided evolution and preference-calibrated candidate prioritization, achieving effective protection under a low query budget. Experiments on three content types and four compression settings show that CAPE improves information loss by up to 75.8% over the strongest baseline while keeping protected content visually indistinguishable from originals. CAPE also transfers to real-world settings, including the LangGraph agent workflow and GitHub Copilot, highlighting its generality and practical value. This paper aims to reveal context compression as a new defense layer, promoting content protection research in the agent era.

</details>


### [143] [MLQENABLER: Enabling Secure Machine Learning Queries over Encrypted Database in Cloud Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08197)
*Xu Zhou,Haoyang Chen,Xinyu Lei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MLQENABLER方案，通过索引辅助方法在加密云数据库上实现安全机器学习查询，在保证可接受安全性的同时仅造成轻微ML性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公有云服务商在提供云存储的同时可利用客户数据提供ML服务，但公有云不可信，存在客户敏感数据泄露风险。简单加密虽能保护数据却使ML无法执行，因此需要一种既能保证安全又能支持ML查询的方案。

Method: 采用索引辅助（index-aid）方法，设计MLQENABLER方案，使得加密数据库在保持伪随机数形式的同时，仍能支持高效的机器学习查询操作。

Result: 初步实验表明，MLQENABLER在达到可接受安全级别的条件下，仅造成轻微的ML性能下降。

Conclusion: MLQENABLER方案能够在加密云存储数据库上实现安全与ML能力的平衡，为公有云场景下的数据安全与智能服务共存提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In cloud computing, the public cloud service providers (CSPs) can provide cloud storage as the primary service while providing additional machine learning (ML)-based services by using the clients' data in storage. This business model extends the border of cloud computing services and brings in new business growth possibilities. Although it is promising, the model also brings in security concerns since the public commercial cloud cannot be fully trusted. For example, the public commercial clouds may sell clients' sensitive data to the government or other companies. To address the security concerns, an immediate solution is to require clients to encrypt their datasets before outsourcing to the cloud. However, if a database is formally encrypted, then the database contains only pseudorandom numbers, making it impossible to enable ML over it. In this project, we propose MLQENABLER (ML Queries Enabler) scheme to enable secure ML queries over encrypted database in cloud storage. MLQENABLER employs an index-aid approach to achieve security and ML capability simultaneously. Our initial experiments show that MLQENABLER achieves an acceptable security level while incurring only a slight ML performance degradation.

</details>


### [144] [Threshold Authorization Without Threshold Signatures: Signature-Agnostic MPC Custody](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08226)
*Dariia Porechna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种双门架构，将成员身份认证与阈值授权解耦，成员使用任意EUF-CMA签名，而阈值授权基于Shamir秘密共享生成密封，实现签名方案与授权逻辑的分离，从而平滑支持后量子密码迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码迁移打破了传统阈值多签模型：基于哈希的签名难以高效支持阈值签署，格基阈值协议仍属新兴研究方向。现有方案在切换到后量子签名时需重新设计整个协议。

Method: 双门架构：第一层（身份认证门）由各成员使用任意EUF-CMA签名方案单独签署批准；第二层（阈值授权门）基于Shamir秘密共享产生的阈值密封，由法定人数联合生成。密封是可编程授权计算的基础实例，支持简单法定人数到更丰富的策略。签名方案作为部署参数，切换仅需密钥轮换而非协议重构。

Result: 实现了签名方案与阈值授权逻辑的解耦；低于阈值的攻击者即使持有t个签名密钥也无法伪造密封，具有信息论安全性；支持使用商用HSM的标准签名API参与；可部署于智能合约、金库模块或HSM等场景。

Conclusion: 该架构为数字资产托管提供了一种后量子就绪的阈值授权方案，将密码学签名迁移简化为密钥轮换操作，无需协议重新设计，且保持了低于阈值的完美保密性。

Abstract: Digital-asset custody has been built on threshold multi-party approval: no operation proceeds unless $t$ of $n$ parties approve, and fewer than t compromised parties can neither authorize nor learn the authorization secret. Threshold signature schemes (TSS) have been the standard mechanism, but the post-quantum transition disrupts this model: standardized hash-based signatures resist efficient threshold signing, and lattice-based threshold protocols remain an emerging research track.
  We present a dual-gate architecture that separates member authentication from threshold authorization. Each member signs its approval with an ordinary signature under any EUF-CMA scheme; the quorum jointly produces a threshold seal from Shamir-shared secrets bound to the operation. The seal is the base instance of a programmable authorization computation: simple quorum is the minimal policy, while richer policies can evaluate secret-shared state without making the member-signature scheme part of that computation. The signature scheme is a deployment parameter: migrating from ECDSA to SLH-DSA or ML-DSA is a key rotation, not a protocol redesign, and members holding keys in commodity HSMs participate through the standard sign API. The architecture can be deployed wherever the asset-control path supports programmable verification, such as smart contracts, vault modules, or HSMs guarding a master key, and produces an enforcement-layer authorization rather than a native chain signature. Below-threshold secrecy is information-theoretic; an adversary holding $\geq t$ signing keys but no coefficient shares still cannot produce the seal.

</details>


### [145] [Simulating the Resident: Generating Executable Smart Home Schedules via LLM Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08231)
*Victor Jüttner,Xenia Wagner,Christoph Jahn,Erik Buchmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）生成多样化的居民角色，与模拟智能家居交互，产生行为驱动的设备交互调度，从而在不依赖侵入式真实数据收集的情况下，为智能家居的安全与隐私研究提供可扩展、隐私保护的实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能家居HCI研究需要真实家庭数据集，但收集此类数据成本高、耗时长且存在严重隐私问题。现有研究缺乏既能反映真实交互行为又能保护隐私的可扩展数据生成方法。

Method: 提出一个三部分框架：(1) 跨五个社会技术维度配置模拟住户的设计框架；(2) 多阶段LLM流水线，生成结构化的、可执行的设备交互调度；(3) 概念验证实验证明可行性。

Result: 通过概念验证展示了该方法能够生成行为合理的交互调度，可在物理测试平台上执行，为无需侵入式真实数据收集的可扩展实验提供了可行性证据。

Conclusion: 该方法有望支持大规模、隐私保护的智能家居实验，减少对真实家庭数据采集的依赖，但仍处于进行中工作阶段。

Abstract: Smart homes have emerged as an important domain for HCI research, including work on usable security and privacy. Ideally, studies in these areas draw on datasets collected in real homes with real residents, capturing authentic device interactions, network traffic, and daily routines. However, creating such datasets is slow, expensive, and raises significant privacy concerns, as it requires long-term observation of people in their most private spaces. We propose using LLMs to generate diverse resident personas that interact with a simulated smart home, producing behaviorally grounded interaction schedules that can be executed on physical testbeds. We present (1) a design framework configuring simulated households across five socio-technical dimensions, (2) a multi-stage LLM pipeline that produces structured, executable device interaction schedules, and (3) a proof of concept demonstrating feasibility. As a work in progress, we aim to support scalable, privacy-conscious smart-home experimentation without relying on intrusive real-world data collection.

</details>


### [146] [Mini-Programs, Mega-Problems: Unveiling OAuth-based Authentication Misuses in Mini-Programs via Dynamic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08232)
