*Zidong Zhang,Zhentao Xie,Lingyun Ying,Qinsheng Hou,Yacong Gu,Wenrui Diao,Jianliang Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现小程序OAuth认证集成中存在三种新型运行时误用模式，设计并实现MINIAUTH框架对44,273个微信和2,721个百度小程序进行分析，发现1,834个误用案例，包括客户端身份伪造、认证绕过等严重逻辑漏洞，并发现百度OBA API的加密设计缺陷，已向开发者及平台负责任的披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要基于静态代码分析检测OAuth集成漏洞，无法处理混淆后的小程序代码，且无法发现运行时动态行为触发的安全缺陷。本文旨在系统性发现和评估小程序OBA运行时误用的实际影响。

Method: 设计并实现MINIAUTH框架，自动定位小程序OBA登录页面，动态执行工作流并分析运行时行为，支持处理混淆小程序，检测三类新型运行时OBA误用。

Result: 对44,273个微信小程序和2,721个百度小程序进行分析，发现1,834个误用案例，包括客户端身份伪造（凭据泄露）、认证绕过（静态/明文标识符）等漏洞，并发现百度OBA API中允许会话密钥暴力破解的加密设计缺陷。

Conclusion: 小程序OBA误用问题跨平台普遍存在，现有防护机制不足，需加强开发者指导与平台级安全管控。

Abstract: Mini-programs have become a dominant paradigm for lightweight application deployment within super apps such as WeChat. To support seamless integration, super apps provide OAuth mechanisms for user login. However, improper integration of OAuth-based Authentication (OBA) flows by third-party developers can lead to critical security flaws. In this paper, we discover three new types of runtime OBA misuses that differ from prior static-code-based studies, enabling attackers to impersonate victims. To assess their real-world impact, we design and implement MINIAUTH, the first analysis framework for systematically analyzing OBA misuse at scale. MINIAUTH automatically pinpoints the OBA login page of a mini-program, executes the workflow dynamically, and analyzes its runtime behaviors. This enables it to handle obfuscated mini-programs and uncover vulnerabilities that existing approaches cannot detect. Applying MINIAUTH to 44,273 WeChat and 2,721 Baidu mini-programs, we uncover 1,834 misuse cases, including critical logic flaws that enable client-side identity forgery via exposed credentials and authentication bypass through static or plaintext identifiers. Our cross-platform evaluation further shows that such misuses are not confined to a single ecosystem but consistently appear across different mini-program platforms. We also identify a cryptographic design flaw in Baidu's OBA APIs that allows brute-forcing of session keys. We responsibly disclosed our findings to the developers and platforms, receiving acknowledgments and assigned CNVD/CNNVD IDs. These results underscore the need for more robust developer guidance and enhanced platform-level safeguards.

</details>


### [147] [Multi-Agent Firewall Architecture for Privacy Protection of Sensitive Data in Interactions with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08282)
*Hugo García Cuesta,Pablo Mateo Torrejón,Alfonso Sánchez-Macián*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种开源、注重隐私、面向用户的防火墙，通过浏览器扩展和代理架构全面拦截HTTP(S)和WebSocket通信，采用多智能体管道结合确定性检测与LLM语义分析实现数据泄露防护，最佳配置下F1分数达94.93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM集成到工作流程中缺乏足够的安全防护，存在显著的数据泄露风险，现有解决方案在隐私保护、开源性和全面性方面存在不足。

Method: 采用浏览器扩展与代理组合的架构实现流量全面拦截；核心为灵活的多智能体管道，通过混合方法（确定性检测器+LLM驱动的语义分析）实现数据泄露防护，并支持专有代码泄露防护及可扩展组件（如提示注入规避）。

Result: 在最优配置下F1分数达到94.93%。

Conclusion: 该分层架构支持异构环境部署，可在计算成本、检测深度和延迟之间灵活平衡，为LLM交互提供有效的开源隐私保护方案。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have become essential productivity tools, their integration into workflows without adequate safeguards creates significant risks. This paper proposes an open-source, privacy-focused, user-facing firewall designed to secure both web-based and programmatic LLM interactions. The architecture combines a browser extension and a proxy for total traffic interception across both HTTP(S) and WebSocket communications. At its core, a flexible multi-agent pipeline delivers data leakage prevention through a hybrid approach combining deterministic detectors with LLM-driven semantic analysis, proprietary code leakage prevention, and extensible components designed for future security enhancements such as prompt injection evasion. The framework's layered architecture enables deployment across heterogeneous environments, allowing organizations to balance computational cost, detection depth and latency. Evaluation results demonstrate it achieves F1 scores of up to 94.93% on optimal configurations.

</details>


### [148] [From Legacy Documentation to OSCAL: An MCP-Based Agent Pipeline for Threat-Informed Continuous Compliance in Critical Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08288)
*Lea Roxanne Muth,Marian Margraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于MCP的多智能体管道，将自然语言系统描述转化为NIST OSCAL格式的知识图谱和合规审计工件，实现非侵入式持续自动化合规管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施的OT环境无法被主动扫描，但又需要进行风险评估和合规性检查，因此需要一种非侵入式的自动化方法。

Method: 构建MCP接地（grounded）的多智能体管道，将LLM推理与确定性知识检索解耦，从权威威胁情报源检索信息，生成NIST OSCAL格式的系统安全计划和安全评估报告。

Result: 在水务设施的合成场景中，CVE召回率达0.90，D3FEND召回率达1.00，生成了符合Schema的OSCAL SSP和SAR。

Conclusion: MCP接地虽不能完全消除幻觉，但将错误约束到资产提取阶段，使剩余风险变得可见、可验证，适合高效的人工审查。

Abstract: In critical infrastructure, operational technology environments often cannot be actively scanned, and yet active system feedback is needed for risk assessment and compliance. This paper presents a non-invasive, MCP-grounded multi-agent pipeline that converts natural-language system descriptions into source-verified knowledge graph and audit-ready artifacts in the NIST OSCAL format for continuous automated compliance management. The architecture decouples LLM-based reasoning from deterministic knowledge retrieval against authoritative threat-intelligence sources, reducing the risk of fabricated vulnerabilities and hallucinated attack paths.
  In an evidence-based synthetic scenario of a water utility, the pipeline achieves 0.90 CVE recall and perfect D3FEND recall. It generates a schema-valid OSCAL System Security Plan and an OSCAL Security Assessment Report. Nevertheless, the core insight is not that grounding via MCP eliminates errors (e.g., hallucinations) entirely from the pipeline, but that it shifts errors into the first phase of asset extraction from the natural language description. Here, a single incorrectly extracted entity can lead to genuine but irrelevant CVEs in subsequent stages of the pipeline, which consumes time and resources. However, it makes the remaining risk visible, verifiable, and suitable for a time-efficient manual review, since the infrastructure (e.g., version numbers, OS, etc.) is typically known.

</details>


### [149] [Reverse Engineering Compliance: A Dual-Graph Verification Framework for Auditing Legacy IT Security Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08292)
*Lea Roxanne Muth,Marian Margraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ASSERT框架，通过本体提取、五类图差异比较和OSCAL导出，实现遗留IT安全文档的自动化验证与合规转换，并在BSI数据集上评估了不同模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIS-2指令要求持续可审计的合规证据，促使从文档合规转向机器可读合规制品。OSCAL是相关标准，但企业仍管理大量遗留IT安全概念（IT-SC），直接迁移可能将过时资产带入新格式。现有研究主要关注新概念生成，缺乏将遗留IT-SC提取为可审计中间表示、确定性比较并导出合规OSCAL工件的验证框架。

Method: 提出ASSERT框架：1）基于本体提取遗留文档为形式化文档图；2）与已验证的参考图进行五类图差异比较；3）导出为符合模式的OSCAL输出。在BSI的RecPlast数据集上，比较本地开放权重模型和商业模型在三种参考本体暴露配置下的表现。

Result: ASSERT能够量化文档-基础设施不一致性，但存在发现未记录实体与强制模式之间的权衡。

Conclusion: ASSERT框架有效实现了遗留IT安全文档的自动化验证与OSCAL合规转换，但需在实体发现完整性与模式合规性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: The NIS-2 Directive increases the need for continuous, auditable compliance evidence and motivates a shift from document-based compliance toward machine-readable compliance artifacts. The Open Security Controls Assessment Language (OSCAL) is a standard for this purpose, which the German Federal Office for Information Security (BSI) is adapting with Grundschutz++. However, companies are still managing extensive legacy IT security concepts (IT-SCs), and migrating them without verification could transfer outdated assets into the new format. While existing research primarily addresses the generation of new concepts, there is a lack of a verification framework that extracts legacy IT-SCs into an auditable intermediate representation, deterministically compares the extracted graph with an independently constructed reference state, and exports schema-valid OSCAL artifacts.
  This paper introduces the Automated Security Concept Structure Extraction and Reverse Topology-checking (ASSERT) Framework, which addresses this gap by using ontology-based extraction of legacy documents into formal document graphs, a five-class graph difference against a verified reference graph, and the export into schema-valid OSCAL outputs for system description and assessment evidence. Using the BSI's RecPlast dataset, we compare a local open-weight model and a commercial model across three configurations with different levels of reference-ontology exposure. The evaluation shows that ASSERT makes document-infrastructure inconsistencies measurable, but reveals a trade-off between discovering undocumented entities and enforcing a schema.

</details>


### [150] [Secure QR Codes: Authenticity Verification via EdDSA Signatures and CBOR Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08383)
*Wojciech Jonderko,Wojciech Wodo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对QR码缺乏安全机制导致的欺骗攻击问题，提出了两种安全QR码架构：基于EdDSA签名和CBOR证书的完全离线方案，以及结合JWKS端点、中央信任注册表和URL片段的可扩展混合Web PKI架构，实现后向兼容和动态实时验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QR码在公共基础设施中广泛使用但缺乏固有安全机制，容易遭受欺骗攻击（如quishing），亟需可部署的安全解决方案。

Method: 首先提出完全离线方案，采用EdDSA签名（Ed25519曲线）、CBOR编码证书和ZLIB压缩，在QR码静态容量内实现密码学完整性；随后提出混合Web PKI架构，利用JWKS端点、中央信任注册表和URL片段，在保持标准相机后向兼容的同时为兼容应用提供动态实时验证。

Result: 离线方案证明可在QR码严格静态容量内实现鲁棒的密码学完整性；混合Web PKI架构解决了离线模型在大型智能城市IoT部署中无法进行即时密钥撤销的可扩展性问题。

Conclusion: 双模式方法为消除QR码欺骗攻击提供了实用、可部署的路径。

Abstract: QR codes are a ubiquitous part of daily life, widely trusted by millions. However, their lack of inherent security features has given rise to critical attack vectors, such as spoofing (quishing) on public infrastructure like self-service parking machines. To address this, we present a comprehensive evolution of secure QR code architectures. First, we evaluate a fully offline proof-of-concept leveraging EdDSA signatures (instantiated on the Ed25519 curve), CBOR-encoded certificates, and ZLIB compression, demonstrating that robust cryptographic integrity can be achieved within the QR code's strict static capacity. However, recognizing the scalability limitations of fully offline models-specifically the inability to perform immediate key revocation in massive smart-city IoT deployments-we subsequently propose a scalable Hybrid Web PKI architecture. This forward-looking model utilizes standardized JWKS endpoints, a Central Trust Registry, and URL fragments to ensure seamless backward compatibility with standard native cameras while providing dynamic, real-time validation for compliant applications. This dual-mode approach offers a practical, deployable path toward eliminating QR spoofing.

</details>


### [151] [Token-Flow Firewall: Semantic Runtime Auditing for Persistent AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08395)
*Puji Wang,Yingchen Zhang,Ruqing Zhang,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TokenWall是一个针对持久化AI代理的运行时防御框架，通过对智能体Token流进行语义审计，将攻击成功率降至12.5%，同时保持97.4%的良性可执行通过率，仅引入0.69秒额外延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化AI代理将大语言模型扩展为长期运行的软件系统，不安全内容可通过持久状态、可复用技能和工具交互传播，形成更大的语义攻击面，亟需有效的运行时安全防御机制。

Method: 提出TokenWall框架，作为语义防火墙对代理Token流进行边界感知审计，构建结构化源-汇审计记录，在执行前进行轻量局部检查，并将高风险歧义案例升级至更强的仲裁模块，实现全覆盖执行前中介。

Result: 在CIK-Bench上，TokenWall将攻击成功率降至12.5%，良性可执行通过率为97.4%，无需人工确认，仅引入0.69秒额外延迟。

Conclusion: 语义运行时包含可以在持久化AI代理中实现实用的安全-效用权衡，TokenWall证明了全覆盖执行前中介在安全防御中的有效性。

Abstract: Persistent AI agents extend large language models (LLMs) beyond single-turn interaction into long-lived software systems. Unlike traditional chat assistants, unsafe content in these agents can propagate through persistent state, reusable skills, and tool-mediated interactions, creating a substantially larger semantic attack surface. We observe that most security-critical interactions in such agents are transmitted through natural-language token flows, including memory updates, tool arguments, retrieved files, and inter-component communications. This observation enables a new security formulation: unsafe behavior can be intercepted as risky semantic flows before reaching privileged runtime sinks. Based on this insight, we propose TokenWall, a runtime defense framework that acts as a semantic firewall over agent token flows. TokenWall performs boundary-aware semantic auditing over these flows, constructing structured source-sink audit records, applying lightweight local inspection before execution, and selectively escalating ambiguous high-risk cases to stronger arbitration modules. Unlike prior approaches that rely on sparse auditing or remote large-model oversight, TokenWall enables full-coverage pre-execution mediation while reducing remote arbitration and latency. Experiments on CIK-Bench show that TokenWall reduces attack success rate to 12.5% while maintaining a 97.4% benign executable pass rate without human confirmation. TokenWall further introduces only 0.69 seconds of additional latency on benign cases, demonstrating that semantic runtime containment can achieve a practical security-utility trade-off for persistent AI agents.

</details>


### [152] [TRACE: A Two-Channel Robust Attribution Watermark via Complementary Embeddings for LLM-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08400)
*Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Xiaoyan Feng,Jiaojiao Jiang,Yang Song,Yulei Sui,Zhenchang Xing,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRACE是首个在代理水印中实现无失真动作选择、自同步删除和重写不变性的方法，通过叠加基于内容的"选择通道"和基于位置的"计数通道"，在工具调用轨迹中嵌入可检测水印，同时保证代理行为分布不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理通过经销商分发时存在归属争议，经销商掌握完整的轨迹日志（工具调用、观察和动作记录），而现有代理水印无法抵御拥有日志完全读写权限的恶意经销商，因其水印可直接从日志中读取或篡改。

Method: TRACE叠加两个水印通道：1) 选择通道——基于局部内容作为密钥，使用无失真采样器选择动作，删除操作不影响同步；2) 计数通道——基于日志骨架（仅位置信息）作为密钥，控制每组决策的记录数量，重写无法改变。两个通道使用不同密钥类型（内容vs位置），分别对抗删除和重写攻击。

Result: 在ToolBench和ALFWorld上，TRACE保持与未加水印代理相同的成功率；选择通道在长轨迹上检测分数接近z=100，在70%步骤删除下仍可检测；计数通道在任意强度的LLM重写下保持完全不变。

Conclusion: TRACE证明行为水印可以通过决策熵换取检测信号，每个决策至少贡献一半熵，确定性决策无贡献；擦除两个通道将迫使经销商破坏其转售的轨迹数据。

Abstract: LLM agents reach users through resellers, who may rebrand a developer's agent or substitute a cheaper model. When provenance is disputed, attribution rests on the trajectory log (the record of tool calls, observations, and executed actions, not the model's reasoning), which the reseller stores and processes to meter usage. A watermark must therefore survive an adversary with full read/write access to the very evidence it is detected from; existing agent watermarks do not, as their attribution is read straight off that log. We present TRACE, to our knowledge the first agent watermark that is distortion-free in its action choices, self-synchronizing under deletion, and unconditionally invariant under rewriting. Deletion desynchronizes a position-derived key and rewriting alters content, so a deletion-robust key must come from content and a rewrite-robust key from position, and no single key serves both. A trajectory, however, has room for two watermarks. TRACE superposes a selection channel that sets which action is chosen, keyed on local content with a distortion-free sampler, so the agent's distribution is provably unchanged and detection resynchronizes after deletions, and a tally channel that sets how many records each decision group holds, keyed on the log's skeleton alone, which no rewriting can touch. We prove this behavioral watermark's signal is bought with decision entropy, each decision paying at least half its entropy and deterministic decisions nothing, and that erasing both channels forces the reseller to corrupt the trajectories it resells. On ToolBench and ALFWorld, TRACE matches the unwatermarked agent's success rate while its selection channel reaches detection scores near z = 100 on long-horizon trajectories, stays detectable under 70% step deletion, and keeps a tally channel exactly unchanged under LLM rewriting of any strength.

</details>


### [153] [TRM-Raft: A Byzantine-Resistant Raft Consensus via Integrated Trust and Reputation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08666)
*Jie Zhang,Xubo Fan,Xiaohong Li,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRM-Raft是一种基于区块链信任与声誉模型的拜占庭容错增强方案，通过非侵入式地将声誉机制集成到Raft共识核心中，在保持低开销的同时有效防御选举伪造、日志篡改等拜占庭行为，在40%拜占庭节点比例下仍能将恶意领导者比例控制在5%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Raft共识因其简洁性和高性能被广泛用于分布式协调（如服务注册、区块链），但仅假设崩溃故障，无法抵御选举伪造和日志篡改等拜占庭行为。现有BFT协议开销过高，而临时性加固缺乏统一防御机制。

Method: 提出TRM-Raft，非侵入式地将基于区块链的信任与声誉模型（B-TRM）集成到Raft共识核心中。具体包括：（1）量化多维度节点行为，应用自适应惩罚区分偶然故障与恶意行为；（2）将声誉嵌入领导者选举和日志复制过程；（3）声誉感知选举惩罚任期/索引伪造，排除低声誉节点的领导权；（4）基于Schnorr签名的机制让追随者验证日志完整性，篡改触发声誉衰减和领导者替换。

Result: 在Hyperledger Fabric上的真实Internetware环境中评估，TRM-Raft在40%拜占庭节点比例下将恶意领导者比例控制在5%以下，吞吐量损失小于10%，延迟增加小于5%。

Conclusion: TRM-Raft为依赖Raft的Internetware系统提供了一条轻量级、实用的可信赖增强路径。

Abstract: Internetware envisions autonomous software entities collaborating over the open Internet. Raft consensus is widely adopted for its simplicity and performance in distributed coordination, e.g., service registries and blockchains. However, Raft assumes crash faults only, making it vulnerable to Byzantine behaviors like election forgery and log tampering. Existing BFT protocols incur high overhead, while ad-hoc hardening lacks unified defense.
  We propose \textbf{TRM-Raft}, a Byzantine-resistant enhancement that non-intrusively integrates a Blockchain-based Trust and Reputation Model (B-TRM) into the consensus core. It quantifies multi-dimensional node behaviors, applies adaptive penalties distinguishing accidental faults from malice, and embeds reputation into leader election and log replication. A reputation-aware election penalizes term/index forgery, excluding low-reputation nodes from leadership. A Schnorr-signature-based mechanism lets followers verify log integrity; tampering triggers reputation decay and leader replacement. Evaluated on Hyperledger Fabric in a realistic Internetware setting, TRM-Raft keeps malicious leader ratio below 5\% even with 40\% Byzantine nodes, with <10\% throughput loss and <5\% latency increase over vanilla Raft. TRM-Raft offers a lightweight, practical trustworthiness path for Internetware systems relying on Raft.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [The Regularization Parameter: Sparse Precision Matrix Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07735)
*Aryan Eftekhari,Daniel Sergio Vega,Ernst-Jan Camiel Wit,Olaf Schenk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需交叉验证的闭式矩阵值正则化参数，用于高维稀疏精度矩阵估计，能够自动平衡欠拟合与过拟合，并在多个数据集上达到与交叉验证相当的精度、更优的支持恢复性能和数量级的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维低样本量数据中，稀疏精度矩阵估计的关键挑战在于如何适当地选择正则化参数，以控制估计器的稀疏性并在欠拟合与过拟合之间取得平衡。现有方法通常依赖交叉验证，计算成本高昂。

Method: 从ℓ1正则化高斯最大似然估计器的一阶最优性条件的采样分布出发，推导出一个闭式矩阵值正则化参数。通过预设重采样下每个非零条目满足其最优性条件的概率，消除了对交叉验证的需求。

Result: 在标准条件下，该正则化参数具有渐近缩放性质，保证了估计器的一致性和稀疏一致性。在合成高斯/非高斯数据集、真实基因芯片和神经影像应用中，达到了与交叉验证相当的估计精度，支持恢复性能更优，运行时间减少了数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该闭式矩阵值正则化参数方法为高维稀疏精度矩阵估计提供了一种高效、无需交叉验证的替代方案，在保持估计精度的同时大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Sparse precision matrix estimation provides an interpretable and computationally efficient framework for modeling conditional dependencies in high-dimensional, low-sample-size data. A recurring challenge is appropriately selecting the regularization parameter that controls estimator sparsity and strikes a balance between underfitting and overfitting. We propose a closed-form, matrix-valued regularization parameter derived from the sampling distribution of the first-order optimality conditions of the $\ell_1$-regularized Gaussian maximum-likelihood estimator. By prescribing the probability that each nonzero entry of the estimator satisfies its optimality condition under resampling, we eliminate the need for cross-validation. The resulting regularization parameter is shown to attain asymptotic scaling properties that, under standard conditions, provide consistency and sparsistency of the estimator. On synthetic Gaussian and non-Gaussian datasets, as well as real-world gene microarray and neuroimaging applications, the proposed approach achieves estimation accuracy comparable to cross-validation, delivers superior support recovery, and reduces runtime by several orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [155] [Distributionally Faithful Imputation via Positive Semi-Definite Kernel Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07767)
*Andrea Basteri,Carlo Ciliberto,Alessandro Rudi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出PSD Impute方法，将MCAR下的缺失值填补重新定义为基于掩码观测的密度估计问题，利用正半定核密度构建凸经验风险问题，通过牛顿内点法求解，实现单重和多重填补，并在理论上具有统计一致性和快速自适应超额风险收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数缺失值填补方法依赖启发式策略或限制性参数假设，忽略了联合数据分布，导致统计推断和机器学习流程受到损害。

Method: 将MCAR下的缺失值填补重新定义为基于掩码观测的密度估计问题；利用正半定核密度构建凸经验风险问题，其边际分布具有闭式解；采用牛顿内点法求解优化问题。

Result: 在1个合成数据集和11个真实世界数据集上的初步实验表明，与流行的填补基线方法相比，PSD Impute在分布准确性上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: PSD Impute方法在理论上具有统计一致性和快速自适应超额风险收敛性（对非常规则的概率分布可打破维度诅咒），并在实证中展现出有竞争力的填补性能，具有强大的实践前景。

Abstract: Missing values undermine statistical inference and machine learning pipelines, yet most imputation methods rely on heuristics or restrictive parametric assumptions that ignore the joint data distribution. We recast imputation under missing completely at random (MCAR) as density estimation from masked observations: estimate a distribution whose observed marginals exactly match those in the data. Leveraging positive semi definite (PSD) kernel densities we obtain a convex empirical risk problem with closed form marginals, solvable by a Newton interior point method. The resulting PSD Impute model yields both single and multiple imputations from the same fitted density, enjoys statistical consistency with fast adaptive excess risk beating the curse of dimensionality for very regular probabilities. Preliminary experiments on one synthetic and eleven real world datasets already indicate competitive distributional accuracy compared with popular imputation baselines, suggesting strong practical promise.

</details>


### [156] [Expressivity and Statistical Trade-offs in Diffusion Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07967)
*Viet Vu,Renyuan Xu,Jiacheng Zhang,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散策略中漂移Lipschitz预算K是控制表达能力和统计行为的关键量。通过理论分析表明，K越大逼近能力越强（误差阶1/K），但统计复杂度也随之增加；两者平衡得到有限样本性能差距阶$\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+6)})$，在耗散漂移类下可改进至$\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+4)})$。实验验证了K的样本依赖权衡，并提出了根据样本量选择K和相应Lipschitz系数神经网络架构的实践原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略虽在实践中展现出对复杂、多模态、非高斯动作分布的表达灵活性，但其表达能力的数学基础尚不清晰，且如何从有限数据中充分利用这一能力也未得到阐明。本文旨在揭示驱动扩散策略表达性的核心数学量，并量化表达性与统计学习之间的权衡关系。

Method: 以漂移Lipschitz预算K为核心量，通过逼近理论量化表达性（扩散策略可集中到最优确定性策略，逼近误差阶为1/K，并给出匹配下界）；通过统计学习理论分析神经网路参数化下的统计复杂度，平衡逼近误差与统计误差得到有限样本泛化界；通过数值实验验证理论预测的样本依赖权衡。

Result: 1) 扩散策略的逼近误差阶为1/K，且下界匹配；2) 有限样本性能差距在通用神经网络下为$\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+6)})$，在单边耗散漂移类下为$\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+4)})$；3) 数值实验验证了K的样本依赖权衡。

Conclusion: 扩散策略的表达性与统计行为由漂移Lipschitz预算K控制，存在逼近-统计的权衡；应根据可用样本量选择K，进而选择对应固定Lipschitz系数的神经网络架构。

Abstract: Diffusion-based policies have recently emerged as powerful policy parameterizations for reinforcement learning, representing state-conditioned action distributions as terminal laws of diffusion processes with parameterized drifts. This terminal-law representation has shown substantial expressive flexibility in practice, enabling diffusion policies to model complex, multimodal, and highly non-Gaussian action distributions; however, it remains unclear what mathematically drives this expressivity and how to fully exploit it when the policy is learned from finite data. In this paper, we identify the drift Lipschitz budget $K$ as a central quantity governing the expressivity and statistical behavior of diffusion policies. We quantify expressivity through approximation: diffusion policies with $K$-Lipschitz drifts can concentrate near optimal deterministic policies and achieve value approximation error of order $1/K$; moreover, we prove a matching lower bound under nondegenerate diffusion noise. This increased expressivity comes with a statistical cost. When the drift is parameterized by neural networks, increasing $K$ improves approximation but increases statistical complexity. Balancing these two terms yields a finite-sample performance gap of order $\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+6)})$ for generic neural-network drifts, and a sharper rate $\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+4)})$ for one-sided dissipative drift classes, where $n$ is the sample size and $m$ is the dimension of the state space. Numerical experiments provide empirical evidence for the sample-dependent trade-off in $K$, supporting both theoretical regimes. Our framework also suggests a practical implementation principle: choose the diffusion budget $K$ according to the available sample size, and then select a neural-network architecture with the corresponding fixed Lipschitz coefficient.

</details>


### [157] [Bayesian Experimental Design via Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08335)
*Angus Phillips,Gavin Kerrigan,Tom Rainforth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将期望信息增益(EIG)的双重难解性与策略学习分离的方法，通过先求解与策略无关的分数匹配问题，再将学习到的分数近似用于单重难解的策略训练，从而将关键的计算成本从乘法变为加法，显著降低策略训练的负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于策略的贝叶斯实验设计(BED)中，期望信息增益(EIG)的双重难解性迫使研究者使用昂贵或复杂的近似方法，限制了策略优化的投入，亟需一种更高效的训练范式。

Method: 1. 将EIG的双重难解性与策略学习解耦；2. 先求解一个与策略无关的分数匹配问题来近似EIG；3. 利用学习到的分数近似将策略训练转化为单重难解问题；4. 将计算成本从乘法(同时处理双重难解性)变为加法(分离处理)。

Result: 在不增加似然评估乘法成本的情况下训练多个竞争性策略，通过选择最佳策略提升性能，即使不进行超参数或架构搜索也能取得更好效果。

Conclusion: 该方法有效降低了BED中策略训练的计算负担，使策略的多次训练(如用于架构搜索、超参数调优或避免局部最优)变得更为经济可行。

Abstract: Policy-based approaches to Bayesian experimental design (BED) allow the learning of deep policy networks that adaptively make intelligent design decisions based on previously collected data. However, the training of such policies is often held back by a fundamental challenge: the double intractability of the expected information gain (EIG). This necessitates expensive or complex approximations that restrict the effort one can invest in optimising the policy itself. To address this, we show that the double intractability of the EIG can be isolated from the policy learning by first solving a score matching problem that is independent of the policy used, then using the learned score approximation to train the policy in a singly intractable manner. This turns the key multiplicative cost into an additive one and reduces the computational burden on the policy training itself, making it far cheaper to train the policy multiple times when needed, e.g. for architecture search, hyperparameter tuning, or avoiding local optima. In our experiments we train multiple competitive policies without inducing a multiplicative cost in likelihood evaluations, which can increase performance by allowing us to select the best policy even without performing hyperparameter or architecture searches.

</details>


### [158] [Prediction-Powered Active Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08347)
*Kianoosh Ashouritaklimi,Valentin Kilian,Daolang Huang,Tom Rainforth,François Caron*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Prediction-Powered Active Testing (PPAT)框架，利用黑盒模型预测作为控制变量来改进主动测试中的风险估计，在保持无偏性的同时降低方差，并设计了新的采样规则和置信区间方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动测试方法未能利用强大黑盒模型的信息性预测来提升标签效率，尽管在标签昂贵的场景下这类预测越来越容易获得。

Method: 将无偏LURE估计器与预测驱动控制变量结合，通过残差化损失函数而非直接使用代理预测作为伪标签来保持无偏性；推导了面向方差缩减的预言机及实用替代采集规则；建立了渐近正态性以得到有效置信区间。

Result: 在表格回归和图像分类任务中，PPAT在风险估计上优于现有方法，其置信区间在更少标签和更小区间宽度下达到目标覆盖水平。

Conclusion: PPAT通过利用黑盒模型预测作为控制变量，显著提升了标签效率，为风险估计提供了更准确且不确定性可量化的解决方案。

Abstract: Active testing provides a label--efficient approach to risk estimation by adaptively selecting which test points should be labelled. However, existing estimators fail to exploit the informative predictions of powerful black--box models, even though such predictions are increasingly available in settings where labels remain expensive. To address this, we propose \textbf{Prediction--Powered Active Testing (PPAT)}, a novel label--efficient risk estimation framework that combines the unbiased LURE estimator \citep{farquhar2021statistical} with a prediction--powered control variate. Rather than using proxy predictions as biased pseudo--labels, PPAT uses them to residualise the loss, preserving unbiasedness while reducing variance. Beyond the estimator itself, PPAT also changes which points should be acquired: we derive oracle and practical surrogate--based acquisition rules tailored to reducing the variance of our estimator. Moreover, we establish asymptotic normality for PPAT, yielding asymptotically valid confidence intervals and thus a principled estimate of the uncertainty around our estimates. Across tabular regression and image--classification tasks, PPAT outperforms existing methods in risk estimation, while its confidence intervals attain the target coverage with substantially fewer labels and smaller widths.

</details>


### [159] [Statistical Efficiency and Inference of Quantile Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08444)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计效率角度系统研究了基于分位数的分布强化学习，证明了在生成模型下，分位数分布策略评估的估计误差为$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{m/n})$，达到了最优参数$\sqrt{n}$收敛率；推导了分位数参数的渐近分布并刻画了半参数效率界；在分位数发散时，渐近协方差结构与半参数效率界匹配，表明分位数估计器在无限维极限下仍保持渐近有效；此外建立了光滑泛函的Berry-Esseen定理，为分位数投影回报分布的统计推断提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习（Distributional RL）虽在实践中表现优异，但其统计效率的理论基础尚不充分。本文旨在从统计效率的视角，严格分析基于分位数的分布策略评估方法的样本效率、渐近性质及最优性，填补该领域的理论空白。

Method: 考虑分位数投影分布贝尔曼方程诱导的分位固定点$\eta_m$，在生成模型假设下基于经验MDP构建估计量$\eta_m^{(n)}$。采用$W_\infty$度量建立非渐近误差界，推导$\sqrt{n}(\theta_m^{(n)}-\theta_m)$的渐近分布并刻画半参数效率界。进一步分析分位数发散时的渐近协方差结构，并与非参数半参数效率界比较。最后建立光滑泛函的Berry-Esseen定理。

Result: (1) 固定分位数$m$时，估计误差为$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{m/n})$，达到最优参数$\sqrt{n}$收敛率；(2) 推导了$\sqrt{n}(\theta_m^{(n)}-\theta_m)$的渐近分布并刻画了半参数效率界，估计量达到该界；(3) 分位数发散时，渐近协方差结构与非参数模型的半参数效率界匹配，证明无限维极限下仍渐近有效；(4) 建立光滑泛函的Berry-Esseen定理，支持有效统计推断。

Conclusion: 基于分位数的分布策略评估方法在统计上具有高效率：固定维数下达到半参数效率界，无限维极限下匹配非参数效率界。本文为分布强化学习的统计推断提供了坚实的理论基础，验证了分位数方法的理论最优性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study quantile-based distributional reinforcement learning from the perspective of statistical efficiency. We focus on distributional policy evaluation, whose goal is to characterize the return distribution, namely the distribution of discounted cumulative rewards under a given policy. To obtain a finite-dimensional representation of the return distribution, we consider the quantile fixed point $η_m$ induced by the quantile-projected distributional Bellman equation. Assuming access to a generative model, we construct an estimator $η_m^{(n)}$ based on an empirical Markov decision process. For a fixed number of quantiles $m$, we establish a non-asymptotic error bound for $η_m^{(n)}$ and $η_m$ under the supremum $W_\infty$ metric, showing that the estimation error scales as $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{m/n})$ with respect to $m$ and $n$. This implies that the quantile-based distributional policy evaluation problem can be solved with sample efficiency, achieving the optimal parametric $\sqrt{n}$ convergence rate. We derive the asymptotic distribution of the quantile parameters $\sqrt{n}(θ_m^{(n)}-θ_m)$ and characterize the semiparametric efficiency bound, which is attained by our estimator. Beyond the fixed-dimensional setting, we investigate the asymptotic regime in which the number of quantiles diverges. We characterize the limit covariance structure and show that it matches the semiparametric efficiency bound of the nonparametric model for distributional policy evaluation, showing that quantile-based estimators remain asymptotically efficient in the infinite-dimensional limit. Finally, we establish a Berry--Esseen theorem for smooth functionals $\sqrt{n}(η_m^{(n)}(s)-η_m(s))f$, thereby providing a foundation for statistically valid inference on functionals of the quantile-projected return distribution.

</details>


### [160] [High-Dimensional Procrustes Matching via Tree Counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08538)
*Xiaochun Niu,Tselil Schramm,Jiaming Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维Procrustes匹配问题，提出了基于加权树计数的多项式时间算法，在常数相关性条件下实现精确恢复，并给出了信息论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Procrustes匹配问题在低维(d=O(log n))已被充分研究，但高维(d≫log n)区域仅知近乎完美相关(ρ=1-o(1))的保证，缺乏常数相关性下的恢复结果。

Method: 算法通过计算和比较特选"宽树"族的加权计数来实现；信息论分析推导了精确恢复的条件；低度优势计算用于分析树计数算法的必要性。

Result: 当d≥polylog(n)且ρ²>√α(α≈0.338为Otter树计数常数)时，算法高概率成功；信息论保证为ρ²≳max{log n/d, √(log n/n)}。

Conclusion: 首次在高维常数相关性下实现精确恢复的多项式时间算法，且ρ²>√α条件可能是树计数算法所必需的。

Abstract: Suppose we observe two sets of $n$ Gaussian vectors in $\mathbb{R}^d$, with the promise that, after applying a permutation of $[n]$ and a rotation of $\mathbb{R}^d$, the two sets are $ρ$-correlated. The Procrustes matching problem asks us to recover the unknown permutation of $[n]$ that aligns the two sets. The problem is well-studied in the low-dimensional regime $d=O(\log n)$, but the high-dimensional regime $d\gg \log n$ has remained largely uncharted: prior matching guarantees require nearly perfect correlation $ρ=1-o(1)$, even for information-theoretic recovery.
  Our main result is a polynomial-time algorithm for exact recovery at constant correlation. The algorithm works by computing and comparing weighted counts of a specially chosen family of ``wide'' trees. So long as $d\ge \mathrm{polylog}(n)$, the algorithm succeeds with high probability for any $ρ^2>\sqrtα$, where $α\approx 0.338$ is Otter's tree-counting constant.
  We complement this algorithmic result with an improved information-theoretic guarantee, showing that exact recovery is possible when $ρ^2 \gtrsim \max\{\log n/d,\sqrt{\log n/n}\}$. We also carry out a low-degree advantage calculation, which suggests that the condition $ρ^2 > \sqrtα$ is necessary for any tree-counting algorithm.

</details>


### [161] [Score Accuracy Along the Forward Diffusion Does Not Certify Numerical Stability in Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08757)
*Yiwei Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散模型中的关键理论问题：即使前向边际误差很小，反向采样的Euler-Maruyama离散化也可能导致所有Wasserstein距离发散（即使弱收敛成立）。作者构造了反例，并提出一种投影方法（将去噪器投影到数据支撑的凸集上）可恢复Wasserstein收敛性，实验验证了该现象与缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分数匹配理论主要分析前向边际误差，但实际采样依赖反向轨迹上的分数评估。本文旨在揭示前向边际误差小不足以保证反向数值采样的稳定性，并探索何时以及为何Wasserstein距离会发散，以及如何修复。

Method: (1) 理论构造：构造光滑分数场，其前向边际L²误差任意小，但Euler-Maruyama离散化的端点所有正阶矩发散，且所有Wasserstein距离W_p (p≥1)发散。(2) 固定架构反例：在有界Lipschitz去噪器族中，前向误差和路径空间总变差趋于0，但离散化端点仍发散。(3) 正结果：对紧支撑数据，将去噪器投影到包含支撑的已知有界闭凸集上，可保持逐点精度、给出网格一致矩界，并在温和局部正则性下恢复Wasserstein收敛。(4) 实验：用固定DiT风格网络验证了稀有轨迹上的矩发散及投影去噪器的抑制效果。

Result: (1) 前向边际L²误差任意小无法保证反向Euler-Maruyama的矩稳定性。(2) 存在路径空间TV收敛但所有Wasserstein距离发散的实例。(3) 去噪器投影到数据支撑凸集可恢复Wasserstein收敛性。(4) 实验证实了理论现象和投影方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的分数匹配理论需关注反向离散化稳定性的新指标，而不仅仅是前向边际误差。投影到支撑凸集是一种简单有效的补救措施，可在保持精度的同时恢复矩和Wasserstein收敛性。

Abstract: Score matching controls average error under the forward marginals, but a discretized reverse-time sampler evaluates the learned score along its own trajectory. We show that small forward-marginal error does not guarantee numerical stability. We construct a single smooth score field with arbitrarily small forward-marginal $L^2$ error. The learned reverse-time process is nonexplosive, has moments of every order, and can be arbitrarily close to the exact reverse-time process in path-space total variation. Yet its Euler--Maruyama discretizations converge in probability while every positive moment diverges. Thus weak convergence can hold even though every Wasserstein distance $W_p$, $p\ge1$, diverges.
  The same failure can occur within one fixed finite neural architecture. We construct a family of bounded, globally Lipschitz denoisers for which both the forward-marginal error and the path-space total variation distance tend to zero, while their Euler--Maruyama endpoints diverge in every $W_p$. For compactly supported data, we also give a simple positive result. Projecting the learned denoiser onto a known bounded closed convex set containing the support preserves pointwise accuracy, gives grid-uniform moment bounds, and yields Wasserstein convergence under mild local regularity. Experiments with a small fixed DiT-style network show large growth along rare numerical trajectories and its suppression by denoiser projection, while overall trajectory errors remain small.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [The logistic-normal integral and the moments of the logistic-normal distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07889)
*Dan Pirjol*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了Logistic-Normal积分的数值计算问题，提出了基于最优级数展开的算法，实现了高精度数值评估并给出了Logistic-Normal随机变量的前两阶矩的显式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Logistic-Normal积分在Logistic模型与高斯随机效应结合的统计估计问题以及广义线性混合模型中频繁出现，但其数值计算存在困难，需要精确、可控误差的数值方法。

Method: 研究了该积分的数值计算及其导数，给出了在某些点上的闭式解和多种级数展开形式，从中筛选出最适合数值计算的最优级数展开，并基于该最优级数设计了具有良好尾部误差控制的算法。

Result: 给出了Logistic-Normal随机变量的前两阶矩（期望和方差）的显式结果。

Conclusion: 提出的基于最优级数展开的算法能够对Logistic-Normal积分进行精确数值评估，并在尾部具有良好的近似误差控制，为相关统计模型的参数估计提供了可靠的计算工具。

Abstract: The logistic-normal integral appears in problems of statistical estimation for logistic models with Gaussian random effects, and generalized linear mixed models. We study the numerical evaluation of this integral and of its derivatives, and give closed form evaluations at certain points and series expansions. There is a continuum of possible series expansions, and we single out one series expansion which is optimal for numerical evaluation. We propose an algorithm for a precise numerical evaluation, based on the optimal series, with good approximation error control in the tails. As an application we give explicit results for the first two moments of a logistic-normal random variable.

</details>


### [163] [From Bayes' Rule to Bayes Rules: Optimal Information Processing and Axiomatic Foundations Beyond Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08019)
*Jeremie Houssineau,Badr-Eddine Chérief-Abdellatif*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了可能性推断（possibilistic inference）的原则性更新规则，其中不确定性通过可能性函数（概率的极大类比）表示，并通过偏序进行逐点比较。从信息守恒和公理化两个互补角度，推导出相同的规范更新形式：后验 = 先验×似然乘积后经上确界归一化。两种推导对任意损失函数均一致，仅在学习率参数的引入方式上存在差异。该参数控制 epistemic strength，且无法仅通过归一化证据识别，从而厘清了类似学习率参数在广义贝叶斯更新中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为可能性推断建立原则性的更新规则，并阐明学习率参数在广义贝叶斯更新中的角色。

Method: 从信息守恒视角和公理化视角两个互补基础出发，推导可能性推断的更新规则。

Result: 两个视角推导出相同的规范更新形式：后验 = 先验×似然乘积后经上确界归一化，仅在损失函数下学习率参数的引入方式不同。

Conclusion: 学习率参数控制 epistemic strength，且无法仅从归一化证据中识别，这澄清了其在广义贝叶斯更新中的作用。

Abstract: This paper develops principled updating rules for possibilistic inference, where uncertainty about a fixed parameter is represented by a possibility function, the maxitive analogue of a probability distribution, and comparisons are made pointwise via a partial order. From two complementary foundations, an information-conservation viewpoint and an axiomatic viewpoint, we derive the same canonical update: the posterior is the prior-likelihood product followed by supremum normalisation. The two derivations agree for an arbitrary loss, differing only in where the learning-rate parameter enters. This parameter controls epistemic strength and is not identifiable from the normalising evidence alone, clarifying the role of analogous learning-rate parameters in generalised Bayesian updating.

</details>


### [164] [Parameter inference for partially observed branching processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08157)
*Simone Baldassarri,Michel Mandjes,Jiesen Wang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个年龄依赖的分支过程，将种群分为幼年和成年两个年龄组，仅利用总种群规模（幼年+成年）的观测数据来估计模型参数。在模型的遍历（ergodic）机制下，引入矩估计方法，并建立了其渐近正态性。还讨论了扩展到多个年龄组的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际的生物学或人口学研究中，往往只能观测到种群的总规模，而无法直接获取各年龄组的详细数据。因此，需要开发一种仅利用总种群规模观测数据就能有效估计年龄依赖分支过程模型参数的方法。

Method: 采用矩估计方法（method-of-moments estimator），在模型的遍历（ergodic）机制下进行参数估计，并证明该估计量的渐近正态性。模型基础设定为将种群分为幼年和成年两个年龄组，并进一步讨论了扩展到更多年龄组的情形。

Result: 成功引入了矩估计量，并在遍历机制下建立了该估计量的渐近正态性。该结果保证了在大样本条件下估计量的统计性质，为仅利用总种群规模数据进行参数推断提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种仅依赖总种群规模观测数据即可估计年龄依赖分支过程模型参数的方法，并通过严格的渐近理论保证了估计量的可靠性。该方法可扩展至多个年龄组的场景，具有一定的普适性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study an age-dependent branching process. In the simplest setting, the population is divided into two age groups, namely juveniles and adults. Our objective is to estimate the model parameters using observations of the total population size only (i.e., juveniles plus adults). Focusing on the ergodic regime of the model, we introduce a method-of-moments estimator and establish its asymptotic normality. Several extensions are discussed, including models with more than two age groups.

</details>


### [165] [An Exact Distribution-Free Test for Means of Nonnegative Random Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08415)
*Nikos Vlassis,Philip S. Thomas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明，当独立非负随机变量 $X_i$ 满足 $\mathbb{E} X_i \le 1$ 时，构造的统计量 $K(X) = \mathbb{P}\{\sum_{i=1}^n x_i D_i \le 1\}$（其中 $D$ 服从均匀Dirichlet分布）满足 $\mathbb{P}\{K(X) \le \alpha\} \le \alpha$，即 $K(X)$ 是检验原假设 $\mathbb{E}X_i \le 1$ 的有限样本、分布自由 $p$-值，证实了Gaffke (2005)的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Gaffke (2005)提出的猜想：存在一个有限样本、分布自由的 $p$-值来检验非负随机变量期望不超过1的原假设。

Method: 利用独立非负随机变量 $X_i$ 与均匀Dirichlet分布 $D$ 的独立性，定义 $K(x)=\mathbb{P}\{\sum_{i=1}^n x_iD_i\le1\}$，并通过概率不等式证明对任意 $n\ge1$，当 $\mathbb{E}X_i\le1$ 时，$\mathbb{P}\{K(X)\le\alpha\}\le\alpha$ 对所有 $\alpha\in[0,1]$ 成立。

Result: 证明了 $K(X)$ 是检验 $\mathbb{E}X_i \le 1$ 的有限样本、分布自由 $p$-值，即 $\mathbb{P}\{K(X) \le \alpha\} \le \alpha$。

Conclusion: 证实了Gaffke (2005)的猜想，为检验非负随机变量期望不超过1提供了有效的统计推断工具。

Abstract: Let $X=(X_1,\ldots,X_n)$ be independent nonnegative random variables, not necessarily identically distributed. Let $D=(D_0,D_1,\ldots,D_n)\sim\operatorname{Dir}(1,\ldots,1)$ be independent of $X$, and define $K(x)=\mathbb{P}\{\sum_{i=1}^n x_iD_i\le1\}$. We prove that, for every $n\ge1$, whenever $\mathbb{E} X_i\le1$ for every $i$, $\mathbb{P}\{K(X)\leα\}\leα$ for all $0\leα\le1$. Thus $K(X)$ is a finite-sample, distribution-free $p$-value for testing the null hypothesis $\mathbb{E}X_i \le 1$ for all $i$. This proves a conjecture of Gaffke (2005).

</details>


### [166] [Building confidence regions for Reeb graphs using the interleaving distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08458)
*Matteo Pegoraro,Alberto Conforti,Mathieu Carrière*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文利用交织距离为Reeb图开发了置信区域，从有限样本中构建了基于Reeb余层的统计估计器，并将Mapper图视为该估计器的受控覆盖粗化，从而分离统计逼近与可视化问题。通过内在/外在度量证明了稳定性界限，并基于标准采样假设和子采样Hausdorff尺度估计推导了置信区域。数值实验展示了如何识别统计显著特征并投影至Mapper图进行解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Reeb图推断方法缺乏严格的统计置信区域，且Mapper图作为可视化工具与底层统计估计器之间的关系尚未被清晰分离和理论化。本文旨在建立从有限样本到Reeb图推断的统计保证框架。

Method: 采用Reeb余层作为主要估计器（基于有限邻近图和线性扩展滤波），将Mapper图视为该估计器的受控覆盖粗化。利用交织距离度量估计误差，在内在度量下和外在度量（正到达条件）下推导稳定性界限。通过(a,b)-标准采样假设或子采样Hausdorff尺度估计构建置信区域，并与扩展持续性伪度量建立联系（上界为2倍交织距离，H_0分量为1倍）。

Result: 证明了Reeb估计器在两种度量下的稳定性界限；建立了扩展持续性伪度量与交织距离之间的定量关系（上界2倍，H_0分量紧界1倍）；给出了基于两种采样假设的置信区域构造方法；数值实验验证了统计显著特征的识别与可视化。

Conclusion: 该方法为Reeb图推断提供了严格的统计置信区域框架，将统计估计与可视化分离，并建立了与持久性拓扑方法之间的理论联系，为实际应用中的特征显著性检验提供了依据。

Abstract: We develop confidence regions for Reeb graphs from finite samples using the interleaving distance. Given a point cloud equipped with a filter function, we construct a finite proximity graph, extend the filter linearly, and use the Reeb cosheaf of the resulting filtered graph as the primary estimator. Mapper graphs are then treated as controlled cover-based coarsenings of this estimator, separating the statistical approximation problem from the visualization problem. We prove stability bounds for the Reeb estimators obtained both using intrinsic and extrinsic metrics, the latter under positive-reach assumptions, and derive interleaving-distance confidence regions from either \((a,b)\)-standard sampling assumptions or subsampling-based Hausdorff scale estimates. We also compare this object-level metric viewpoint with persistence-based guarantees by showing that the extended-persistence pseudometric is bounded by twice the interleaving distance, with sharp constant \(1\) for the \(H_0\)-related components. Numerical experiments illustrate how statistically significant features can be identified and then projected to Mapper graphs for interpretation.

</details>


### [167] [A screening approach to nonparametric inference from the M/G/1 workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08472)
*Royi Jacobovic,Binyamin Kobzantsev*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对Hansen和Pitts (2006)提出的M/G/1工作负荷模型中服务时间分布的非参数推断这一长期开放问题，提出了一种仅基于观测工作负荷轨迹的估计量B_n(w)，在无需平稳性、稳定性或已知到达率的条件下，达到了参数化的L^1风险率O(log n/√n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Hansen和Pitts (2006)提出的关于M/G/1工作负荷模型中服务时间分布的非参数推断的长期开放问题，在不假设稳定性或平稳性的条件下进行统计推断。

Method: 提出估计量B_n(w)，通过筛选机制从工作负荷过程中提取条件独立的复合泊松增量，将依赖数据问题转化为拉普拉斯变换反演框架，仅基于观测的工作负荷轨迹进行估计。

Result: 在B(·)连续可微、在w处二次可微且具有有限二阶矩的条件下，建立了期望误差界：E|B_n(w)-B(w)| = O(log n/√n)，首次实现了该问题的参数化L^1风险率（至多相差对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为Hansen-Pitts问题提供了不依赖平稳性、稳定性或到达率知识即可达到参数化L^1风险率的解决方案。

Abstract: We address a long-standing open problem posed by Hansen and Pitts (2006) on nonparametric inference for the service-time distribution in an M/G/1 workload model. We consider an M/G/1 queue with unknown arrival rate $λ>0$ and service-time distribution $B(\cdot)$, without assuming stability or stationarity. A statistician observes the workload process at discrete times $t=0,1,\ldots,n$ and aims to estimate $B(w)$ at a fixed point $w>0$.
  We propose an estimator $B_n(w)$ based solely on the observed workload trajectory. The construction relies on a screening mechanism that extracts conditionally i.i.d. compound Poisson increments from the workload process, thereby reducing the dependent-data problem to a Laplace-transform inversion framework.
  Under mild regularity assumptions on $B(\cdot)$, i.e., continuous differentiability on $[0,\infty)$, twice differentiability at $w$, and a finite second moment, we establish the bound \[ \mathbb{E}\bigl|B_n(w)-B(w)\bigr| =\mathcal{O}\!\left(\frac{\log n}{\sqrt{n}}\right), \qquad n\to\infty. \]This provides the first solution to the Hansen-Pitts problem achieving a parametric $L^1$-risk rate (up to a logarithmic factor), without requiring stationarity, stability, or knowledge of the arrival rate.

</details>


### [168] [Functional dependence and synchronous coupling in ergodic autoregressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08567)
*Paul Doukhan,Lionel Truquet*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文指出，非线性时间序列分析中常用的函数依赖度量依赖于特定的创新表示，但该表示对应的概率空间可能与模型自然的动态概率空间不一致。对于一类均匀遍历的自回归过程，当模型参数变化时，自然创新耦合的行为会发生质变，这种转变与李雅普诺夫指数的符号变化吻合。正李雅普诺夫指数可能阻碍相同创新驱动下轨迹对初始扰动的遗忘，尽管马尔可夫链本身是均匀遍历的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出函数依赖度量在非线性时间序列分析中应用时，其依赖的创新表示所对应的概率空间可能与模型自然的动态概率空间不一致，从而影响度量的解释和适用性。

Method: 通过分析一类均匀遍历的自回归过程，研究模型参数变化时自然创新耦合行为的质变，并将其与李雅普诺夫指数的符号变化相关联。

Result: 发现自然创新耦合的行为在参数变化时发生质变，该转变与李雅普诺夫指数符号变化一致；正李雅普诺夫指数会阻止相同创新驱动下轨迹对初始扰动的遗忘，尽管马尔可夫链是均匀遍历的。

Conclusion: 强调了在解释或应用函数依赖度量时，必须仔细指定底层概率空间的重要性。

Abstract: Functional dependence measures have become an important tool in the analysis of nonlinear time series and are typically formulated with respect to a given innovation representation of the process. This note points out that the probability space on which such representations yield the expected memory loss properties may not always coincide with the natural dynamical probability space of the model. We exhibit classes of uniformly ergodic autoregressive processes for which the behavior of the natural innovation coupling undergoes a qualitative transition as the model parameter varies. For this family of models, this transition coincides with a change in the sign of an associated Lyapunov exponent. In particular, a positive Lyapunov exponent may prevent the forgetting of initial perturbations along trajectories driven by the same innovations, despite uniform ergodicity of the associated Markov chain. These observations highlight the importance of carefully specifying the underlying probability space when interpreting or applying functional dependence measures.

</details>


### [169] [Exact Permutation Recovery Under Unknown Scalar Affine Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08628)
*Tigran Galstyan,Avetik Karagulyan,Arshak Minasyan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究在未知标量仿射变换下两组含噪特征向量的匹配问题，提出先标准化估计仿射变换、再通过最小化对数最小二乘（LSL）估计排列的两步方法。理论表明，当特征向量最小间隔距离达到一定阈值时，排列可完美恢复，该阈值在温和异方差下与非仿射场景一致。同时证明存在需要更大间隔距离的配置，使得该匹配问题在极小极大意义下更具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在未知标量仿射变换下两组含噪特征向量的匹配问题，探究该场景下排列恢复的理论极限。

Method: 两步法：先标准化特征向量估计未知标量仿射变换，再利用估计出的变换通过最小化对数最小二乘（LSL）估计排列。

Result: 当真实特征向量的最小间隔距离达到 $\sqrt{ρ_σ} \vee (d\log n)^{1/4} \vee \sqrt{\log n}$ 时，未知排列可完美恢复；存在需要更大间隔距离的配置，表明该问题在极小极大意义下比非仿射设置更具挑战性。

Conclusion: 特征匹配中标准化数据可隐式估计标量仿射参数；证明了异方差噪声下仿射参数估计量的非渐近浓度界。

Abstract: We study the problem of matching two sets of noisy feature vectors when underlying true features are related by an unknown scalar affine transformation. Our method comprises two primary steps. First, we standardize the feature vectors to estimate the unknown scalar affine transformation. Subsequently, we estimate the permutation by minimizing the Least Sum of Logarithms (LSL) between two sets of observations using the estimated transformation.
  Our main result shows that the unknown permutation can be perfectly recovered given that the minimal separation distance of true feature vectors scales as $\sqrt{ρ_σ} \vee (d\log n)^{1/4} \vee \sqrt{\log n}$, where $d$ is the ambient dimension, $n$ is the sample size, and $ρ_σ$ is the maximal ratio of noise magnitudes. Interestingly, the obtained rate, under mild heteroscedasticity, coincides with that of the non-affine setting. We additionally demonstrate that there exist configurations requiring a larger minimal separation distance for perfect recovery. The latter makes the matching problem more challenging from minimax perspective compared to the non-affine setting.
  Consequently, we show that in the problem of feature matching, standardizing the data implicitly estimates the scalar affine parameters. As part of our analysis, we prove non-asymptotic concentration bounds for the affine parameter estimators in the presence of heterogeneous noise magnitudes.

</details>


### [170] [Low-Rank Matrix Recovery via Heavy-Tailed Quadratic Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08671)
*Gao Huang,Song Li*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明了在采样向量元素仅具有有限4+δ矩的弱假设下，核范数最小化和半定约束经验风险最小化两种凸方法能以最优样本复杂度m=O(rn)实现低秩埃尔米特矩阵的均匀、稳定、鲁棒恢复，填补了重尾采样场景下理论分析的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩矩阵恢复理论大多依赖高斯或亚高斯假设，但实际应用中采样向量常呈现重尾分布，而该场景下的理论理解非常匮乏，因此需要桥接这一理论空白。

Method: 利用解耦技术建立的二次型矩估计，结合重尾背景下协方差估计的最新进展，分析了核范数最小化和半定约束经验风险最小化两种凸方法。

Result: 在采样向量元素仅具有有限4+δ矩的弱假设下，两种方法实现了最优样本复杂度m=O(rn)（依赖于矩的常数），并获得了均匀、稳定、鲁棒的恢复保证。同时，在复射影4-设计采样下改进了低秩矩阵恢复的样本复杂度，并在弱矩假设下获得了相位检索的稳定性保证。

Conclusion: 本文成功将低秩矩阵恢复的理论保证从高斯/亚高斯假设拓展到仅需有限4+δ矩的重尾场景，显著放宽了理论所需的假设条件。

Abstract: The problem of recovering an (approximately) low-rank Hermitian matrix $\pmb{M}_0 \in \mathbb{C}^{n \times n}$ of rank $r$ from quadratic sampling matrices of the form $\{\pmb{a}_k \pmb{a}_k^*\}_{k=1}^m$ arises in a variety of applications, including phase retrieval. To obtain rigorous recovery guarantees, the sampling vectors $\{\pmb{a}_k\}_{k=1}^m$ are typically modeled probabilistically. However, most existing theoretical results rely on Gaussian or sub-Gaussian assumptions, which may not accurately capture practical data models. In many applications, sampling vectors exhibit heavier tails, while theoretical understanding in such regimes remains scarce.
  In this paper, we bridge this gap. We show that two widely used convex approaches, nuclear norm minimization and semidefinite-constrained empirical risk minimization, achieve uniform, stable, and robust recovery under the mild assumption that the entries of the sampling vectors have only finite $4+δ$ moments, with the optimal sample complexity $m = \mathcal{O}(rn)$ up to moment-dependent constants. The two main ingredients of our analysis are moment estimates for quadratic forms established via decoupling, together with recent advances in covariance estimation in heavy-tailed settings. As byproducts, we also establish the optimal sample complexity for low-rank matrix recovery under complex projective $4$-design sampling, thereby improving upon previous results, and obtain stability guarantees for phase retrieval under similarly weak moment assumptions.

</details>


### [171] [Statistical inverse learning problems with random observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.15341)
*Abhishake Rastogi,Tapio Helin,Nicole Mücke*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文综述了随机实验设计下统计逆问题的最新进展，涵盖线性和非线性问题，讨论了谱正则化、投影正则化和凸罚正则化等方法，在希尔伯特尺度下分析了收敛率，并展示了在药代动力学/药效学（PK/PD）模型中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计逆问题在随机实验设计下具有重要的理论意义和实际应用价值，但此前缺乏系统性综述。本文旨在整合近年来在谱正则化、投影正则化及凸罚正则化等方面的研究成果，为相关领域提供统一的理论框架。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了线性与非线性统计逆问题的正则化技术，包括谱正则化、投影正则化（在希尔伯特尺度下提出新见解）以及凸罚正则化。利用再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）结构推导样本量意义下的收敛率，并给出极小极大最优性分析。

Result: 获得了概率意义下基于样本量的收敛率，在期望和概率两个层面上均达到了极小极大最优速率。在投影正则化方面提出了新的理论见解，并明确了证明所需的关键假设条件。

Conclusion: 本文为随机实验设计下的统计逆问题提供了统一的理论综述，证明了多种正则化方法的最优收敛性质，并通过PK/PD非线性逆问题实例展示了方法的实际应用价值。

Abstract: We provide an overview of recent progress in statistical inverse problems with random experimental design, covering both linear and nonlinear inverse problems. Different regularization schemes have been studied to produce robust and stable solutions. We discuss recent results in spectral regularization methods and regularization by projection, exploring both approaches within the context of Hilbert scales and presenting new insights particularly in regularization by projection. Additionally, we overview recent advancements in regularization using convex penalties. Convergence rates are analyzed in terms of the sample size in a probabilistic sense, yielding minimax rates in both expectation and probability. To achieve these results, the structure of reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces is leveraged to establish minimax rates in the statistical learning setting. We detail the assumptions underpinning these key elements of our proofs. Finally, we demonstrate the application of these concepts to nonlinear inverse problems in pharmacokinetic/pharmacodynamic (PK/PD) models, where the task is to predict changes in drug concentrations in patients.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [LiST: Lipschitz Scaling Training for Robust and Calibrated Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07745)
*Arthur Chiron,Franck Mamalet,Thomas Massena,Thomas Deltort,Mathieu Serrurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST)，通过将Lipschitz约束与温度缩放（Temperature Scaling）理论联系起来，自动选择最优Lipschitz常数L*，使得网络同时具备准确性、鲁棒性和校准性，并在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny-ImageNet上验证了有效性和样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确性、鲁棒性和校准性是可靠神经网络的三大核心要求，但现有研究往往单独处理这些方面。Lipschitz约束模型虽能通过设计保证鲁棒性，但手动选择Lipschitz常数L会带来准确性-鲁棒性的权衡，且其校准性质尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，寻找一个能同时满足这三者的统一框架。

Method: 1) 理论上建立强制Lipschitz约束与温度缩放（Temperature Scaling）之间的联系，证明存在一个非平凡的最优Lipschitz常数L*；2) 提出Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST)，通过迭代调整全局Lipschitz常数以自动达到校准点；3) 在训练损失中引入margin参数，构建完全校准的帕累托前沿，使用户能在校准状态下遍历准确性-鲁棒性权衡；4) 在收敛后将校准数据重新整合到训练中，提升样本效率。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny-ImageNet数据集上，LiST在保持校准性的同时，达到了与有约束和无约束基线方法可比的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 校准性可作为选择准确性-鲁棒性帕累托前沿上明确定义的工作点的原则性准则；LiST能够在不牺牲校准性的前提下提升样本效率，为构建同时满足三要素的可靠神经网络提供了有效范式。

Abstract: While accuracy, robustness, and calibration are all essential for reliable neural networks, they are often studied separately; developing models that satisfy all three simultaneously remains a central challenge. Lipschitz-constrained models guarantee robustness by design, yet the manual selection of the Lipschitz constraint L governs the resulting accuracy-robustness trade-off, and their calibration properties remain largely underexplored. In this work, we highlight a theoretical and empirical link between the enforced Lipschitz constraint and Temperature Scaling, a state-of-the-art calibration method. Specifically, we find that for a given training scheme, there exists a non-trivial value L* that yields an out-of-the-box calibrated network, and that calibration acts as a principled criterion to select a well-defined operating point on the accuracy-robustness Pareto front. Leveraging these insights, we introduce Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST), a novel training paradigm that iteratively adjusts the global Lipschitz constant to reach this operating point. Through a margin parameter in the training loss, LiST further enables the construction of a fully calibrated Pareto front, allowing users to navigate the accuracy-robustness trade-off while remaining calibrated throughout. At convergence, LiST also enables the reintegration of calibration data into training, improving sample efficiency without sacrificing calibration. We validate LiST on CIFAR-10/100 and Tiny-ImageNet, demonstrating competitive accuracy and robustness against constrained and unconstrained baselines, while remaining calibrated out of the box. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [173] [A law of robustness for two-layer neural networks with arbitrary weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07778)
*Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Bubeck-Li-Nagaraj关于两层神经网络拟合噪声标签时Lipschitz常数下界的猜想（至多差一个对数因子），适用于连续分段线性激活函数（如ReLU）。关键创新在于用函数空间覆盖替代参数空间覆盖，并引入刚性引理：在d≥3的球面或球体上，每个规范扭结的系数受Lipschitz常数控制。d=2时刚性引理失效，存在显式反例匹配猜想端点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明两层神经网络（权重无界）在拟合噪声标签时，Lipschitz常数必须随样本量n和宽度m满足下界Ω(√(n/m))。此前Bubeck-Sellke仅能证明有界参数情形，且深度≥3时必然需要有界性假设。

Method: 1) 函数空间覆盖替代参数空间覆盖（因权重无界无法用参数覆盖）
2) 刚性引理：在d≥3的球面/球体上，不同超平面上的扭结在一般位置不会抵消，从而每个扭结系数受Lipschitz常数控制
3) 将网络函数分解为规范扭结的线性组合，利用刚性引理将拟合条件转化为Lipschitz下界

Result: 对任意连续分段线性激活函数，数据均匀来自S^{d-1}(d≥3)或N(0,I_d/d)，标签噪声σ²>0，宽度m的两层网络（任意实数权重、偏置、跳跃连接）以高概率满足：
Lip(f) ≥ c ε √(n/(\bar m log(C\bar m nd/ε)))，其中\bar m=(K-1)m+1。
还给出了基于实现扭结数k(f)的版本：Lip(f) ≥ c ε √(n/((k(f)+1) log(...)))。

Conclusion: 验证了Bubeck-Li-Nagaraj猜想（至多对数因子），揭示了无界权重情形下函数空间结构（扭结刚性）而非参数范数控制泛化边界。d=2时反例表明该下界在欠参数化端是紧的。

Abstract: Bubeck, Li and Nagaraj conjectured that, for generic data, any two-layer neural network with $m$ neurons that fits $n$ noisy labels must have Lipschitz constant at least of order $\sqrt{n/m}$, with no restriction on the size of the weights. Bubeck and Sellke proved a universal version of this law for Lipschitz-parameterized classes, but under a polynomial bound on the parameters; at depth three that boundedness hypothesis is genuinely necessary. The two-layer unbounded-weight case requires a different argument. We prove the conjectured law, up to one logarithmic factor, for every continuous piecewise-linear activation, in particular for ReLU networks. For data drawn uniformly from $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$, $d\ge3$, or from $N(0,I_d/d)$, labels in $[-1,1]$ with noise level $σ^2>0$, and any width-$m$ two-layer network with arbitrary real weights, biases and affine skip connection, fitting the data $\varepsilon$ below the noise floor forces $\mathrm{Lip}(f)\ge c\,\varepsilon\sqrt{n/(\bar m\log(C\bar m nd/\varepsilon))}$, $\bar m=(K-1)m+1$, with high probability. A realized-kink-count version holds on the same event: every realized two-layer piecewise-linear function with $k(f)\le n$ distinct kink hyperplanes obeys the bound with $\bar m$ replaced by $k(f)+1$, irrespective of how many redundant hidden units parameterize it. The proof replaces parameter-space covering, impossible for unbounded weights, by a function-space covering. The central deterministic ingredient is a rigidity lemma: on $B_2$, and on $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ for $d\ge3$, the coefficient of each canonical kink is controlled by the Lipschitz constant of the realized function, because kinks on distinct hyperplanes cannot cancel at generic points. Rigidity genuinely fails at $d=2$, and an explicit two-layer ReLU interpolant with $O(1)$ Lipschitz constant at width $2n$ matches the law at the overparameterized endpoint.

</details>


### [174] [Reinforcing the Generation Order of Multimodal Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08056)
*Yidong Ouyang,Zhe Wang,Sourav Bhabesh,Dmitriy Bespalov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过GRPO学习可控生成顺序模块，在文本到图像生成和多模态理解任务中分别提升4.08%和4.85%的相对性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明自适应生成顺序在数学推理和代码生成中有效，但在文本到图像生成和多模态理解中，仅靠模型logits不足以确定最优生成顺序，因此需要更有效的顺序控制方法。

Method: 引入一个可学习的控制模块，使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 训练来确定生成顺序，而非依赖模型logits。

Result: 在GenEval基准上实现4.08%相对改进，在VLMEvalKit上实现4.85%相对改进。

Conclusion: 学习生成顺序控制模块能显著提升扩散语言模型在文本到图像对齐和多模态理解方面的能力，尤其在细粒度空间关系捕捉和多模态推理任务上表现突出。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently achieved substantial progress in natural language generation tasks. Recent research demonstrates that adaptive token generation ordering can significantly improve performance in mathematical reasoning and code synthesis applications. In this work, we investigate the optimization of generation order for both text-to-image synthesis and multimodal understanding. We first establish that, unlike structured problems in language generation such as Sudoku puzzles, model logits alone are insufficient for determining optimal generation sequences in text-to-image generation and multimodal understanding. To address this challenge, we introduce a learnable control module trained via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to determine the generation order. Our results demonstrate that learning this control block substantially improves both text-to-image alignment and multimodal understanding in DLMs. In particular, it enhances the model's ability to capture fine-grained spatial relationships in generated images while also strengthening performance on multimodal reasoning and comprehension tasks. We evaluate our framework on GenEval, an object-focused benchmark for text-to-image alignment, where it achieves 4.08% relative improvements. In addition, experiments on VLMEvalKit confirm 4.85% relative improvements in multimodal understanding, highlighting the broad effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [175] [Structure Learning on Clustered Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08238)
*Ryan Thompson,Matt P. Wand,Veerabhadran Baladandayuthapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种可处理聚类数据的DAG结构学习方法，通过引入混合效应框架和可微图耦合机制，在保证全局无环性的同时估计局部簇级效应，并提供收敛性保证和渐近一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DAG结构学习方法假设总体同质，无法处理存在簇级变异（如患者特异性效应）的聚类数据。

Method: 将经典混合模型中的固定效应和随机效应框架扩展到结构学习，设计可微图耦合机制确保固定效应图与随机效应图的并集保持无环，提出一阶收敛方法并利用跨簇批量更新。

Result: 在合成和真实数据上，该方法能检测到替代估计器遗漏的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 证明了模型可辨识性和渐近恢复真实结构的能力，凸显了在聚类设置中结构学习的价值。

Abstract: Recent algorithmic advances have made directed acyclic graph (DAG) structure learning scalable for causal discovery. Yet, the currently available techniques assume a completely homogeneous population, precluding their application to clustered data where cluster-specific variations (e.g., patient-specific effects) are common. We address this issue by introducing a new approach that estimates a global structure while accounting for local cluster-level effects. The key idea is to extend the fixed- and random-effects framework of classical mixed models to the structure learning setting. Towards this end, we present a differentiable graph coupling mechanism that guarantees the union of the fixed- and random-effects graphs remains acyclic. Computationally, we provide a provably convergent first-order method and leverage efficient batched updates across clusters. Statistically, we establish identifiability of the model and show that our approach recovers the true structure asymptotically. In experiments on real and synthetic data, our proposal detects dependencies missed by alternative estimators, underscoring its value for structure learning in clustered settings.

</details>


### [176] [An interpretable Good--Turing restart criterion for k-means++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08243)
*Renato Cordeiro de Amorim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GTRC（Good-Turing重启准则），通过结合Good-Turing估计、无条件界和置信界来动态决定k-means++算法的重启次数，而非传统固定的任意重启次数。实验表明，GTRC能在达到同等聚类质量的同时，根据数据集难度自适应调整重启次数，提供了一个有原则且可报告的重启策略替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-means++算法常通过多次重启来避免局部最优，但重启次数几乎总是随意选择且对所有数据集统一使用，这导致：1) 基于该选择的比较不可靠；2) 在简单数据集上浪费计算资源；3) 在困难数据集上可能重启不足。

Method: GTRC结合了三个组件：1) Good-Turing估计——估计未观察到的结果概率；2) 一个经过证明的无条件界；3) 一个基于置信度的界——计算再次重启能改进当前结果的概率，当该概率低于用户指定的容忍度ε时停止重启。

Result: 在36个数据集上，GTRC达到了与精心选择的固定重启次数相当的聚类质量，同时其使用的重启次数随数据集难度显著且适当地变化，由可解释的数据依赖信号驱动，而非固定规则。

Conclusion: GTRC为提前固定k-means++重启次数提供了一种有原则且可报告的替代方案，能够根据数据集的难度自适应调整计算资源，避免了盲目均匀分配重启次数带来的问题。

Abstract: The k-means++ algorithm is commonly restarted multiple times to avoid poor local optima, yet the number of restarts is almost always chosen arbitrarily and applied uniformly regardless of data set difficulty. This undermines any comparison relying on such a choice and wastes computation on easy data sets while potentially under-serving hard ones. We introduce GTRC, a restart criterion combining a Good-Turing estimate, a proven unconditional bound, and a confidence-based bound on the probability that a further restart would improve on the current result, stopping once this probability falls below a user-specified tolerance $\varepsilon$. Across 36 data sets, GTRC reached clustering quality competitive with well-chosen fixed restart counts, while the number of restarts used varied considerably and appropriately with data set difficulty, governed by an interpretable, data-dependent signal rather than a fixed rule. GTRC offers a principled and reportable alternative to fixing the number of $k$-means++ restarts in advance. Software:https://github.com/RCdeAmorim/Good-Turing-Restart-Criterion.

</details>


### [177] [AutoAnchor: Stable Diffusion Unlearning Using Cross-Attention as a Manifold Surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08337)
*Siyuan Wen,Jiahao Zeng,Ningning Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MYSYS（两阶段框架），通过自动合成流形近端锚点来解决扩散模型遗忘中的不稳定问题，利用交叉注意力一致性损失作为流形近似的高效代理，在概念移除和非目标效用上分别提升高达31.04%和4.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散遗忘方法存在缺陷：基于锚点的方法依赖人工选择的语义锚点，存在有偏遗忘风险；无锚点方法因潜在更新无约束而面临不稳健的遗忘问题。作者希望实现稳定、无偏且高效的扩散模型遗忘。

Method: 在流形假设下理论形式化了不稳定遗忘问题，证明缺乏流形近端锚点会导致显著的法向空间漂移。提出两阶段框架MYSYS：自动合成流形近端锚点，并设计交叉注意力一致性损失作为流形邻近度的高效代理项，避免直接几何流形优化的计算难题。

Result: 在多个SOTA基线方法上，MYSYS实现鲁棒且无偏的遗忘：目标概念移除提升31.04%（CLIP分数），非目标效用提升4.18%（CLIP分数）。集成到现有遗忘方法后，概念移除平均提升6.30%，效用平均提升6.65%。

Conclusion: MYSYS通过理论驱动的流形近端锚点自动合成与高效代理损失，有效解决了扩散遗忘的不稳定问题，且具有良好的可扩展性，能作为即插即用模块增强现有方法。

Abstract: Diffusion unlearning is essential for mitigating the generation of harmful or copyrighted content in text-to-image models. Current diffusion unlearning techniques determine the model update direction by either using alternatives of the target concept as an anchor or using empty prompts. The anchor-based method relies on manually and semantically-chosen anchors that risk biased unlearning, while the anchor-free method inherently suffers from unrobust unlearning due to unconstrained latent updates. In this work, we theoretically formalize such unstable diffusion unlearning issues under the manifold hypothesis and prove that lacking a manifold-proximal anchor inevitably induces significant normal-space drift that degrades unlearning performance. To achieve stable unlearning, we propose \mysysn, a two-stage framework that automatically synthesizes manifold-proximal anchors. However, direct geometric manifold optimization is computationally intractable. To address this challenge, \mysys introduces a novel cross-attention consistency loss which serves as a highly efficient surrogate of manifold proximity. Experimental results demonstrate that \mysys effectively achieves robust and unbiased unlearning across various state-of-the-art baselines, significantly improving targeted concept removal (by up to 31.04\% in CLIP score) and non-target utility (by up to 4.18\% in CLIP score). Moreover, \mysys can also be easily integrated into existing diffusion unlearning methods to enhance their unlearning performance (by 6.30\% for concept removal and 6.65\% for utility on average).

</details>


### [178] [Beyond Backpropagation: Monte Carlo Method Can Train Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08406)
*Hong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明最简单的蒙特卡洛算法——随机突变一个参数，若损失下降则保留，否则重试——可以在单GPU上有效训练深层网络，无需梯度、批归一化或残差连接，并支持剪枝训练、离散权重、非常规激活函数等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播依赖梯度带来固有的梯度消失/爆炸问题，追求无梯度方法一直是AI领域的目标。

Method: 最简单的蒙特卡洛算法：随机突变一个参数，如果损失下降则保留该突变，否则回退并重试。

Result: 成功训练20层以上的深度网络、单隐层宽网络（最多16384个隐藏神经元）以及简单的Transformer架构（MNIST图像分类和Tiny Shakespeare字符级语言建模），无需批归一化或残差连接。

Conclusion: 这种简单的无梯度方法为理解神经网络的自组织和学习机制提供了补充视角，也为构建物理启发的深度学习系统提供了替代路径。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) dominates deep learning training, but its reliance on gradients brings inherent troubles -- vanishing and exploding gradients. The pursuit of gradient-free methods has long been a goal in the field of artificial intelligence. This paper shows that indeed the simplest Monte Carlo algorithm implemented on a single GPU -- randomly mutate a parameter, keep it if the loss decreases, otherwise retry -- can practically train deep networks. This gradient-free method does not even need common techniques such as batch normalization or residual connections to directly train sufficiently deep networks. More remarkably, its flexibility extends to several nontrivial scenarios: it enables pure pruning training, supports discrete weights, accommodates unconventional transfer functions such as Gaussian, and reveals the substantial redundancy of deep networks. We have demonstrated its feasibility on deep networks with more than 20 layers, single-hidden-layer wide networks with up to 16,384 hidden neurons, and even a simple Transformer architecture trained on both image classification (MNIST) and character-level language modeling (Tiny Shakespeare). This simple gradient-free method may offer a complementary perspective for understanding the self-organization and learning mechanisms of neural networks, and also provides an alternative route for building physically inspired deep learning systems.

</details>


### [179] [Towards the Explainability of Temporal Graph Networks via Memory Backtracking and Topological Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07716)
*Yazheng Liu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MemExplainer，通过拓扑归因树和记忆回溯树两种结构，结合LRP归因方法，为时序图神经网络（TGNs）提供忠实的事后解释，解决了现有方法忽略记忆模块中历史事件影响的问题，并在九个数序图数据集上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序图神经网络（TGNs）在预测中表现优异，但其核心的记忆模块（记录和更新节点历史信息）对预测的影响尚未被现有解释方法探索，缺乏对历史事件如何驱动模型预测的深入理解。

Method: 通过拓扑归因树捕获邻居及其记忆向量的影响，再通过记忆回溯树量化历史事件如何塑造节点记忆向量；在TGNs中应用LRP确保事件总贡献等于模型logits；设计优化目标解决top-k选择因logits到概率的非线性映射导致的不忠实问题。

Result: 在九个时序图数据集上（涵盖节点属性预测、链路预测和图分类任务），该方法提供了忠实解释，并优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 提出的解释方法有效补足了TGNs可解释性中对记忆模块的缺失，通过双树结构和LRP实现忠实归因，同时解决了top-k选择不忠实的问题。

Abstract: Temporal graphs are ubiquitous in real-world applications and Temporal Graph Networks (TGNs) have achieved superior predictive accuracy. Understanding which historical events drive model predictions can enhance trustworthiness of TGNs. Existing explanation methods overlook the memory module, the core component that records and updates node histories, leaving the influence of past events unexplored. To address this, we attribute TGNs predictions through the topology attribution tree and memory backtracking tree. The topology attribution tree captures the influence of neighbors and their memory vectors, then the memory backtracking tree quantifies how historical events shape node memory vectors. We apply the LRP in TGNs, ensuring that the total contribution of events equals the logits of model. Finally, top-k selection may be unfaithful due to the nonlinear mapping from logits to probabilities, we design optimization objectives to identify the important events. Experiments on nine temporal graph datasets, spanning node property prediction, link prediction tasks and graph classification tasks, show that our method provides faithful explanations and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/yazhengliu/MemExplainer

</details>


### [180] [Who Gets Missed in the Tail? Thresholded Subgroup Underdiagnosis in Long-Tailed Chest X-ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07717)
*Ha-Hieu Pham,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Dang P. M. Cao,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Min Xu,Trung-Nghia Le,Ulas Bagci,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在胸部X光分类任务中研究了罕见阳性患者的公平性问题，通过诊断阶梯方法（包括长尾损失、子群加权、组鲁棒性和阈值选择）对模型进行审计。实验表明，结合组尾加权和尾感知阈值能显著降低尾部FNR和最差组FNR，但残留漏诊率仍然较高。研究结论：罕见标签的公平性取决于发现、子群和操作阈值的联合作用，而非仅依赖标签频率或排序指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在胸部X光分类中，即使整体排序性能可接受，仍可能导致罕见阳性患者（尤其是子群内）被遗漏在阈值之下。本文旨在部署前对此公平性问题进行审计，探究谁被模型遗漏。

Method: 使用诊断阶梯方法，依次分离类别级长尾损失、子群感知加权、组鲁棒性和阈值选择。在VinDr-CXR和MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT数据集上进行实验，采用组尾加权、尾感知阈值化、配对bootstrap对比和GroupDRO参考运行等方法。

Result: 在VinDr-CXR上，组尾加权+尾感知阈值将尾部FNR从0.665降至0.269，性别最差组FNR从0.705降至0.157，年龄最差组FNR从0.822降至0.133，macro-mAP从0.611提升至0.635。在MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT上，尾部FNR从0.866降至0.741，各子群最差组FNR均下降，但残留漏诊率仍较高。

Conclusion: 罕见标签的公平性依赖于发现类别、子群和操作阈值的联合作用，而非仅靠标签频率或排序指标。聚合的组鲁棒性本身无法消除罕见子群的漏诊问题，支持狭义审计声明。

Abstract: In chest X-ray (CXR) classification, acceptable ranking performance can still leave rare-positive patients below threshold, especially within subgroups. We study this pre-deployment fairness problem as an audit question: after a long-tailed multi-label CXR model is converted from scores into decisions, who is missed? Across VinDr-CXR and MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, we use a diagnostic ladder to separate class-level long-tail losses, subgroup-aware weighting, group robustness, and threshold selection. On VinDr-CXR, group-tail weighting followed by tail-aware thresholding reduces tail FNR from 0.665 to 0.269, sex worst-group FNR from 0.705 to 0.157, and age worst-group FNR from 0.822 to 0.133, while macro-mAP increases from 0.611 to 0.635. On MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, the same score-to-threshold comparison reduces tail FNR from 0.866 to 0.741 and lowers worst-group FNR across sex, age, race, and insurance; residual missed-positive rates nevertheless remain high. Paired bootstrap contrasts on VinDr support the thresholded FNR reductions, and GroupDRO reference runs indicate that aggregate group robustness alone does not remove rare subgroup misses in this setting. The study supports a narrow audit claim: rare-label fairness in CXR depends jointly on the finding, subgroup, and operating threshold, not on label frequency or ranking metrics alone.

</details>


### [181] [LLT: Local Linear Transformer for PDE Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07718)
*Oded Ovadia,Eli Turkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部线性Transformer（LLT），通过结合线性全局注意力与局部空间混合，并融入坐标和几何信息，解决了标准注意力在PDE算子学习中的二次复杂度和缺乏局部偏置问题。在弹性、塑性、翼型流、管道流、达西流及3D汽车空气动力学等多个问题上，LLT相比现有基线取得了有竞争力的精度，并在结构化网格上训练速度提升1.8-2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer注意力在PDE算子学习中存在两个主要局限：一是计算复杂度随计算节点数二次增长，二是缺乏对局部交互的显式偏置。这促使作者设计更高效且适合PDE问题的注意力机制。

Method: LLT架构结合了线性全局注意力（降低复杂度）与局部空间混合（引入局部偏置），并显式融入坐标与几何信息，以更好地处理不同离散化方案（有限元、有限体积、有限差分）和网格类型（结构化/非结构化）上的PDE问题。

Result: 在弹性、塑性、翼型流、管道流、达西流等多个PDE基准上，LLT取得了与现有神经算子和Transformer基线相比有竞争力或更低的相对L2误差。在结构化网格上，每训练迭代的壁钟时间相比Transolver降低了1.8-2.5倍。该方法还成功扩展到包含32,186个非结构化网格点的3D汽车空气动力学数据集。

Conclusion: LLT提供了一种准确且计算高效的PDE算子学习方法，适用于多种离散化方式、网格类型和问题设置，在精度和效率上均表现出优势。

Abstract: Neural operators have become a common approach for learning PDE solution maps and accelerating numerical simulations. Transformer-based neural operators are of particular interest, since attention can learn long-range dependencies in the computational domain. However, standard attention has two major limitations when applied to PDEs: it scales quadratically with the number of computational nodes, and it lacks an explicit bias toward local interactions. To address these issues, we introduce Local Linear Transformer (LLT) for PDE operator learning. The architecture combines linear global attention with local spatial mixing, and incorporates coordinate and geometry information. We evaluate LLT on several PDE problems, including elasticity, plasticity, airfoil flow, pipe flow, and Darcy flow. The reference data for these problems span finite-element, finite-volume, and finite-difference discretizations on structured and unstructured meshes. Compared with other neural-operator and transformer baselines from prior studies, LLT achieves competitive or lower relative $L_2$ error across these problems. On matched structured discretizations, wall-clock time per training iteration is reduced by factors of 1.8 to 2.5 relative to Transolver. We also scale the approach and apply it to a three-dimensional car aerodynamics dataset with 32,186 unstructured mesh points per sample. Together, these results indicate that LLT provides an accurate and computationally efficient operator for PDE problems across discretizations, mesh types, and problem settings.

</details>


### [182] [ReCoLoRA: Spectrum-Aware Recursive Consolidation for Continual LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07719)
*Wentao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCoLoRA是一种面向持续微调的谱感知框架，通过递归合并低秩适配器，在每个新任务开始时重新分解当前有效权重，而非原始权重，从而避免任务间灾难性遗忘。在六个任务的持续GLUE序列上，ReCoLoRA在四个7-8B基座模型中的三个上取得了最佳平均分数，且训练参数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA风格的参数高效微调方法在单任务上表现良好，但在持续任务序列中，每个新任务会不断在冻结权重上堆叠低秩更新，导致旧任务知识被覆盖（灾难性遗忘）。现有方法缺乏对权重谱结构的感知和利用。

Method: 1) 从预训练权重的随机SVD初始化适配器；2) 通过肘部准则选择每层有效秩；3) 先适配主子空间，再开放残差容量；4) 每新任务前，将当前有效权重（而非原始权重）重新分解为冻结残差、缓慢更新的主成分和新的适配器（递归合并）。

Result: 在六个任务的持续GLUE序列上，使用四个7-8B基座模型，ReCoLoRA在三个基座上取得了最佳平均分数，优于LoRA、PiSSA、AdaLoRA和DoRA基线，且训练参数更少。oracle路由的任务库变体作为完全任务隔离下的上界。

Conclusion: ReCoLoRA通过递归合并和谱感知策略有效缓解了持续微调中的灾难性遗忘，在参数效率和任务性能之间取得了更好的平衡，代码已开源。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts a large language model to one task cheaply, but across a task sequence LoRA-style methods keep stacking low-rank updates on the same frozen weight, so each new task tends to overwrite the previous ones. We present ReCoLoRA (Recursive Consolidation of Low-Rank Adapters), a spectrum-aware framework for continual fine-tuning: adapters are initialized from a randomized SVD of the pretrained weight, per-layer effective ranks are selected by an elbow criterion, and the principal subspace is adapted before residual capacity is opened. Before each new task, ReCoLoRA re-decomposes the current effective weight, rather than the original one, into a frozen residual, a slowly updated principal component, and a fresh adapter (recursive consolidation), so every task starts from the model that has already absorbed its predecessors. On a six-task continual GLUE sequence over four 7-8B backbones, ReCoLoRA attains the best final average score on three of the four backbones against rank-swept LoRA, PiSSA, AdaLoRA, and DoRA baselines while training fewer parameters; an oracle-routed task-bank variant serves as an upper bound under full task isolation. Code: https://github.com/bhqy666/ReCoLoRA.

</details>


### [183] [Omni-Sleep: A Sleep Foundation Model via Hierarchical Contrastive Learning of CNS--ANS Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07720)
*Zhoujie Hou,Song Wang,Kexin Lou,Mo Wang,Chen Wei,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Omni-Sleep是一个利用中枢神经系统/自主神经系统分区作为生理先验的睡眠基础模型，通过对超过10万小时多中心多模态PSG数据进行预训练，在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务上优于现有基线模型，展现了更好的标签效率、跨数据集泛化能力和对缺失模态的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有睡眠基础模型通常以拓扑无关的方式融合异质性生物信号，忽略了信号的生理组织方式（即CNS与ANS的协调动力学），导致表征学习缺乏结构约束。

Method: 提出Omni-Sleep模型，以CNS/ANS分区作为生理先验进行拓扑约束表征学习。包含三个学习目标：系统内一致性（捕获子系统级共享因素）、系统间同步（对齐子系统轨迹建模脑-体动力学）、潜空间掩码时序建模（捕获长程睡眠动力学）。在超10万小时多中心多模态PSG数据上预训练。

Result: 在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务上，Omni-Sleep在多种数据集和模态消融设置下均优于强基础模型基线，展现出更好的标签效率、跨数据集泛化能力和对缺失模态的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 生理层次结构对可泛化的睡眠表征学习具有重要价值，Omni-Sleep验证了将领域先验（CNS/ANS分区）融入基础模型设计的有效性。

Abstract: Sleep physiology arises from the coordinated dynamics of the central nervous system (CNS) and autonomic nervous system (ANS), as reflected by multimodal polysomnography signals including EEG, EOG, EMG, ECG, and respiration. However, existing sleep foundation models often fuse heterogeneous biosignals in a topology-agnostic manner, overlooking their physiological organization. We introduce Omni-Sleep, a sleep foundation model that uses the CNS/ANS partition as a physiological prior for topology-constrained representation learning.
